
===== FILE backend/tests/test_mysql_integration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import json
import os
import stat
from io import StringIO
from pathlib import Path
from subprocess import CompletedProcess
from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Any, Sequence

import pytest

from app import security


def _module() -> Any:
    try:
        return importlib.import_module("app.mysql_integration")
    except ModuleNotFoundError:
        pytest.fail("app.mysql_integration 尚未实现", pytrace=False)


def _integration_root(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration" / "mysql84"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    return volume, root


def _prepare_safe_module(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> tuple[Any, Path]:
    module = _module()
    volume, root = _integration_root(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "MYSQL_INTEGRATION_ROOT", root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    return module, root


def _fake_binaries(module: Any, tmp_path: Path) -> Any:
    prefix = tmp_path / "mysql84"
    binary_dir = prefix / "bin"
    binary_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    for name in ("mysqld", "mysql", "mysqladmin"):
        path = binary_dir / name
        path.write_text("#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n", encoding="utf-8")
        path.chmod(0o755)
    return module.MySQLBinaries.for_prefix(prefix)


def test_mysql_integration_paths_are_confined_to_mysql84_directory(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.assert_safe()

    assert paths.root == root
    assert paths.datadir == root / "data"
    assert paths.socket_dir == root / "socket"
    assert paths.socket == root / "socket" / "mysql.sock"
    assert paths.logs == root / "logs"
    assert paths.checkpoints == root / "checkpoints"
    assert paths.reports == root / "reports"
    assert paths.config == root / "my.cnf"
    assert paths.pid_file == root / "mysql.pid"
    assert paths.accounts_file == root / "accounts.json"
    assert paths.port == 33084


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "unsafe_suffix",
    [
        (),
        ("3d66-label-system-integration",),
        ("unrelated-project", "mysql84"),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_integration_paths_reject_broad_or_wrong_roots(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    unsafe_suffix: tuple[str, ...],
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    volume = root.parents[1]
    unsafe = volume.joinpath(*unsafe_suffix)

    with pytest.raises(module.IntegrationPathError, match="MySQL 8.4"):
        module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(unsafe).assert_safe()


def test_mysql_integration_paths_reject_symlinked_child(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    outside = tmp_path / "outside"
    outside.mkdir()
    (root / "logs").symlink_to(outside, target_is_directory=True)

    with pytest.raises(module.IntegrationPathError, match="符号链接"):
        module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root).assert_safe()


def test_mysql_disk_report_enforces_project_and_free_space_limits(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    (root / "small.bin").write_bytes(b"1234567")

    report = module.integration_disk_report(
        module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    )

    assert report == {
        "root": str(root.resolve()),
        "project_used_bytes": 7,
        "project_limit_bytes": 120 * 1024**3,
        "filesystem_total_bytes": 500 * 1024**3,
        "filesystem_free_bytes": 300 * 1024**3,
        "minimum_free_bytes": 40 * 1024**3,
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("project_bytes", "free_bytes", "message"),
    [
        (120 * 1024**3, 300 * 1024**3, "120 GiB"),
        (1, 40 * 1024**3 - 1, "40 GiB"),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_disk_report_fails_closed_at_capacity_limits(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    project_bytes: int,
    free_bytes: int,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_directory_size", lambda _root: project_bytes)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=500 * 1024**3 - free_bytes,
            free=free_bytes,
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(module.IntegrationCapacityError, match=message):
        module.integration_disk_report(
            module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
        )


def test_initialize_mysql_writes_private_local_only_configuration(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    binaries = _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    def fake_run(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
        commands.append(tuple(command))
        if tuple(command[1:]) == ("--version",):
            return CompletedProcess(
                command,
                0,
                "mysqld  Ver 8.4.11 for macos15.0 on arm64 (Homebrew)",
                "",
            )
        if "--initialize-insecure" in command:
            (root / "data" / "auto.cnf").write_text(
                "[auto]\nserver-uuid=fixed-test-uuid\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
        return CompletedProcess(command, 0, "", "")

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_port_available", lambda _port: True)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    module.initialize_mysql(paths, binaries, run_command=fake_run)

    assert len(commands) == 2
    assert commands[0] == (str(binaries.mysqld), "--version")
    assert commands[1][0] == str(binaries.mysqld)
    assert f"--defaults-file={paths.config}" in commands[1]
    assert "--initialize-insecure" in commands[1]
    rendered = paths.config.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "bind-address=127.0.0.1" in rendered
    assert "port=33084" in rendered
    assert f"socket={paths.socket}" in rendered
    assert "mysqlx=0" in rendered
    assert "default-storage-engine=InnoDB" in rendered
    assert "transaction-isolation=READ-COMMITTED" in rendered
    assert "character-set-server=utf8mb4" in rendered
    assert "collation-server=utf8mb4_0900_as_cs" in rendered
    assert "default-time-zone=+00:00" in rendered
    assert "local-infile=0" in rendered
    assert "symbolic-links=0" in rendered
    assert "log-bin-trust-function-creators=ON" in rendered
    assert "binlog-expire-logs-auto-purge=ON" in rendered
    assert "binlog-expire-logs-seconds=86400" in rendered
    assert paths.config.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    assert paths.root.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o700
    marker = json.loads(paths.instance_marker.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert marker["format"] == "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1"
    assert marker["port"] == 33084
    assert marker["version"] == "8.4.11"


def test_initialize_mysql_is_idempotent_for_the_same_verified_instance(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.datadir.mkdir()
    paths.instance_marker.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1",
                "root": str(root.resolve()),
                "port": 33084,
                "version": "8.4.11",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    module.initialize_mysql(
        paths,
        _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path),
        run_command=lambda command: commands.append(tuple(command)),
    )

    assert commands == []


def test_initialize_mysql_rejects_wrong_binary_series(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    binaries = _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)

    def fake_run(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
        return CompletedProcess(
            command,
            0,
            "mysqld  Ver 9.6.0 for macos15.0 on arm64 (Homebrew)",
            "",
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_port_available", lambda _port: True)
    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="MySQL 8.4"):
        module.initialize_mysql(
            module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
            binaries,
            run_command=fake_run,
        )

    assert not (root / "data").exists()


def test_initialize_mysql_rejects_occupied_integration_port(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    binaries = _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_port_available", lambda _port: False)
    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="33084"):
        module.initialize_mysql(
            module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
            binaries,
            run_command=lambda command: CompletedProcess(
                command,
                0,
                "mysqld  Ver 8.4.11 for macos15.0 on arm64 (Homebrew)",
                "",
            ),
        )

    assert not (root / "data").exists()


def test_preflight_mysql_requires_apfs_and_reports_mysql_84(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    binaries = _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)
    filesystem_probes: list[Path] = []

    report = module.preflight_mysql(
        module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
        binaries,
        filesystem_type=lambda path: filesystem_probes.append(path) or "apfs",
        run_command=lambda command: CompletedProcess(
            command,
            0,
            "mysqld  Ver 8.4.11 for macos15.0 on arm64 (Homebrew)",
            "",
        ),
    )

    assert report["filesystem"] == "apfs"
    assert report["mysql_version"] == "8.4.11"
    assert report["local_only"] is True
    assert report["port"] == 33084
    assert filesystem_probes == [root.parents[1].resolve()]


def test_preflight_mysql_rejects_non_apfs_volume(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="APFS"):
        module.preflight_mysql(
            module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
            _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path),
            filesystem_type=lambda _path: "exfat",
            run_command=lambda _command: pytest.fail(
                "文件系统不合格时不应探测 MySQL 版本"
            ),
        )


def test_start_and_stop_use_exact_config_and_validated_pid(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.datadir.mkdir()
    paths.config.write_text("[mysqld]\n", encoding="utf-8")
    paths.instance_marker.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1",
                "root": str(root.resolve()),
                "port": 33084,
                "version": "8.4.11",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    binaries = _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)
    started: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    class FakeProcess:
        pid = 43210

    def fake_start(command: Sequence[str], **_kwargs: object) -> FakeProcess:
        started.append(tuple(command))
        paths.pid_file.write_text("43210\n", encoding="ascii")
        return FakeProcess()

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_wait_for_mysql_ready", lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None)
    module.start_mysql(paths, binaries, start_process=fake_start)

    assert started == [
        (
            str(binaries.mysqld),
            f"--defaults-file={paths.config}",
            "--daemonize",
        )
    ]
    terminated: list[int] = []
    module.stop_mysql(
        paths,
        process_command=lambda _pid: (
            f"{binaries.mysqld} --defaults-file={paths.config} --daemonize"
        ),
        terminate_pid=terminated.append,
    )
    assert terminated == [43210]


def test_stop_mysql_refuses_stale_pid_for_another_process(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.datadir.mkdir()
    paths.config.write_text("[mysqld]\n", encoding="utf-8")
    paths.pid_file.write_text("43210\n", encoding="ascii")
    paths.instance_marker.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1",
                "root": str(root.resolve()),
                "port": 33084,
                "version": "8.4.11",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    terminated: list[int] = []

    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="不属于"):
        module.stop_mysql(
            paths,
            process_command=lambda _pid: "/usr/bin/python unrelated.py",
            terminate_pid=terminated.append,
        )

    assert terminated == []


def test_mysql_status_identifies_only_the_project_instance(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.datadir.mkdir()
    paths.config.write_text("[mysqld]\n", encoding="utf-8")
    paths.pid_file.write_text("43210\n", encoding="ascii")
    paths.instance_marker.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-instance-v1",
                "root": str(root.resolve()),
                "port": 33084,
                "version": "8.4.11",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    status = module.mysql_status(
        paths,
        process_command=lambda _pid: (
            f"/opt/homebrew/opt/mysql@8.4/bin/mysqld "
            f"--defaults-file={paths.config} --daemonize"
        ),
    )

    assert status == {
        "running": True,
        "pid": 43210,
        "root": str(root),
        "socket": str(paths.socket),
        "port": 33084,
    }


def test_cli_preflight_outputs_machine_readable_report(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    prefix = tmp_path / "mysql84"
    _fake_binaries(module, tmp_path)
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.cli_main(
        [
            "preflight",
            "--root",
            str(root),
            "--mysql-prefix",
            str(prefix),
        ],
        filesystem_type=lambda _path: "apfs",
        run_command=lambda command: CompletedProcess(
            command,
            0,
            "mysqld  Ver 8.4.11 for macos15.0 on arm64 (Homebrew)",
            "",
        ),
        output=output,
    )

    assert exit_code == 0
    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    assert payload["command"] == "preflight"
    assert payload["status"] == "ok"
    assert payload["root"] == str(root.resolve())
    assert payload["filesystem"] == "apfs"
    assert payload["mysql_version"] == "8.4.11"


class _SQLRecorder:
    dialect = SimpleNamespace(name="mysql")

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, object]] = []

    def exec_driver_sql(
        self,
        statement: str,
        parameters: object = None,
    ) -> _ScalarResult:
        # SQLAlchemy hands PyMySQL an empty mapping for parameterless driver SQL;
        # PyMySQL still applies percent interpolation in that case.
        if parameters is None:
            statement = statement % {}
        self.calls.append((statement, parameters))
        return _ScalarResult(None)


class _ScalarResult:
    def __init__(self, value: object):
        self.value = value

    def scalar_one(self) -> object:
        return self.value


def test_bootstrap_accounts_store_only_keychain_references(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    recorder = _SQLRecorder()
    secrets = iter(("root-secret", "migration-secret", "app-secret", "test-secret"))
    protected: dict[str, str] = {}

    def protect(value: str, *, account: str) -> str:
        protected[account] = value
        return f"keychain:v1:{account}"

    references = module.bootstrap_mysql_accounts(
        recorder,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        paths,
        protect_secret_fn=protect,
        password_factory=lambda: next(secrets),
    )

    rendered_sql = "\n".join(statement for statement, _params in recorder.calls)
    rendered_parameters = repr([params for _statement, params in recorder.calls])
    assert "root-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "migration-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "app-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "test-secret" not in rendered_sql
    assert "root-secret" in rendered_parameters
    assert " ON *.* " not in rendered_sql
    assert "`labelsystem\\_integration\\_%`.*" in rendered_sql
    assert "`labelsystem\\_test\\_%`.*" in rendered_sql
    assert references.migration_password_reference.startswith("keychain:v1:")
    payload = paths.accounts_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "root-secret" not in payload
    assert "migration-secret" not in payload
    assert "app-secret" not in payload
    assert "test-secret" not in payload
    assert '"format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1"' in payload
    assert paths.accounts_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == (
        stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "account",
    (
        "mysql84-root",
        "mysql84-migration",
        "mysql84-app",
        "mysql84-test-admin",
    ),
)
def test_mysql_account_names_are_supported_by_real_secret_storage(
    account: str,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    stored: dict[str, str] = {}

    class FakeKeychain:
        def set_secret(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:
            stored[key] = value

    monkeypatch.setattr(security.sys, "platform", "darwin")
    monkeypatch.setattr(security, "_get_macos_keychain", FakeKeychain)

    reference = security.protect_secret("fixed-test-secret", account=account)

    assert reference == f"keychain:v1:{account}"
    assert stored == {account: "fixed-test-secret"}


def test_database_url_resolves_secret_in_memory_without_changing_accounts_file(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.accounts_file.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "socket": str(paths.socket),
                "port": 33084,
                "migration_user": "labelsystem_migration",
                "migration_password_reference": "keychain:v1:migration",
                "app_user": "labelsystem_app",
                "app_password_reference": "keychain:v1:app",
                "test_user": "labelsystem_test_admin",
                "test_password_reference": "keychain:v1:test",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.chmod(0o600)
    original = paths.accounts_file.read_bytes()
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "unprotect_secret", lambda _reference: "resolved-secret")

    url = module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
        paths.accounts_file,
        role="migration",
        database="mysql",
    )

    assert url.drivername == "mysql+pymysql"
    assert url.username == "labelsystem_migration"
    assert url.password == "resolved-secret"
    assert url.database == "mysql"
    assert url.query["unix_socket"] == str(paths.socket)
    assert paths.accounts_file.read_bytes() == original


def test_database_url_supports_server_maintenance_without_default_schema(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.accounts_file.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "socket": str(paths.socket),
                "port": 33084,
                "test_user": "labelsystem_test_admin",
                "test_password_reference": "keychain:v1:test",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.chmod(0o600)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "unprotect_secret", lambda _reference: "secret")

    url = module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
        paths.accounts_file,
        role="test",
        database=None,
    )

    assert url.database is None
    assert url.username == "labelsystem_test_admin"


def test_database_url_supports_root_maintenance_without_exposing_secret(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.accounts_file.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "socket": str(paths.socket),
                "port": 33084,
                "root_user": "root",
                "root_password_reference": "keychain:v1:root",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.chmod(0o600)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "unprotect_secret",
        lambda _reference: "must-not-be-rendered",
    )

    url = module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
        paths.accounts_file,
        role="root",
        database=None,
    )

    assert url.username == "root"
    assert url.database is None
    assert url.password == "must-not-be-rendered"
    assert "must-not-be-rendered" not in str(url)


def test_accounts_loader_rejects_symlink(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    outside = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    outside.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "password": "plaintext-is-forbidden",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.symlink_to(outside)

    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="普通文件"):
        module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
            paths.accounts_file,
            role="migration",
            database="mysql",
        )


def test_accounts_loader_rejects_plaintext_password_fields(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.accounts_file.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "password": "plaintext-is-forbidden",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.chmod(0o600)

    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="明文"):
        module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
            paths.accounts_file,
            role="migration",
            database="mysql",
        )


def test_accounts_loader_rejects_overly_broad_file_permissions(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module, root = _prepare_safe_module(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    paths = module.MySQLIntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    paths.accounts_file.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": "labelsystem-mysql84-accounts-v1",
                "socket": str(paths.socket),
                "port": 33084,
                "migration_user": "labelsystem_migration",
                "migration_password_reference": "keychain:v1:migration",
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    paths.accounts_file.chmod(0o644)

    with pytest.raises(module.MySQLIntegrationError, match="0600"):
        module.mysql_database_url_from_references(
            paths.accounts_file,
            role="migration",
            database="mysql",
        )

_NOW = datetime(2026, 8, 28, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _partial_unique_row(table_name: str, **overrides: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    rows: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
        "category_evaluation_v3_revisions": {
            "category_key": "partial-category",
            "display_name": "条件唯一类目",
            "revision": 1,
            "status": "active",
            "contract_json": "{}",
            "classification_map_json": "{}",
            "subcategory_dimensions_json": "{}",
            "contract_hash": "a" * 64,
        },
        "evaluation_jobs": {
            "asset_id": 9001,
            "regression_item_id": 9101,
            "baseline_regression_item_id": None,
            "strategy_bundle_id": 9201,
            "technical_attempt": 0,
        },
        "source_identity_verifications": {
            "contract_key": "partial-source-contract",
            "source_system": "partial-source",
            "key_fields_json": "[]",
            "result": "verified",
            "probe_hash": "b" * 64,
            "data_window": "2026-08-28",
            "scoped_row_count": 1,
            "duplicate_key_count": 0,
            "res_id_conflict_count": 0,
            "status": "approved",
            "created_by": "mysql-invariant-test",
        },
        "content_records": {
            "source_system": "partial-content-source",
            "source_content_id": "partial-content-1",
            "category_key": "partial-category",
            "source_version": "v1",
            "source_occurred_at": _NOW,
            "content_key": "partial-content-key",
            "identity_status": "verified",
            "status": "ready",
        },
        "tag_demand_contracts": {
            "contract_key": "partial-tag-contract",
            "version": 1,
            "status": "active",
            "definition_json": "{}",
            "contract_hash": "c" * 64,
        },
        "semantic_tag_facts": {
            "asset_version_id": 9301,
            "field_key": "partial-field",
            "fact_version": 1,
            "field_status": "required",
            "values_json": "[]",
            "evidence_json": "[]",
            "source_evaluation_id": 9401,
            "source_review_id": 9501,
            "contract_id": 9601,
            "normalization_version": "v1",
            "mapping_version": "v1",
            "status": "approved",
            "payload_hash": "d" * 64,
        },
        "baseline_truth_revisions": {
            "baseline_set_item_id": 9701,
            "revision": 1,
            "truth_kind": "structured_fields",
            "expected_level": None,
            "expected_result_json": "{}",
            "truth_contract_key": "partial-truth-contract",
            "workflow_input_json": "{}",
            "source_correction_key": "partial-correction-key",
            "reason": "验证条件唯一约束",
            "actor": "mysql-invariant-test",
            "previous_hash": "0" * 64,
            "canonical_hash": "e" * 64,
        },
    }
    row = dict(rows[table_name])
    row.update(overrides)
    return row


def _insert_core_row(connection, table_name: str, **values: object) -> None:
    connection.execute(
        Base.metadata.tables[table_name].insert(),
        _partial_unique_row(table_name, **values),
    )


def _assert_insert_rejected(connection, table_name: str, **values: object) -> None:
    transaction = connection.begin()
    try:
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            _insert_core_row(connection, table_name, **values)
    finally:
        transaction.rollback()


def _insert_and_commit(connection, table_name: str, **values: object) -> None:
    with connection.begin():
        _insert_core_row(connection, table_name, **values)


def _final_sqlite_trigger_names() -> set[str]:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="labelsystem-mysql-invariant-inventory-"
    ) as temporary:
        database = Path(temporary) / "inventory.db"
        engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{database}")
        try:
            Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
            with engine.begin() as connection:
                run_migrations(connection)
            with engine.connect() as connection:
                return {
                    str(row[0])
                    for row in connection.execute(
                        text(
                            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master "
                            "WHERE type='trigger'"
                        )
                    )
                }
        finally:
            engine.dispose()


def test_invariant_catalog_covers_every_final_sqlite_trigger() -> None:
    sqlite_triggers = _final_sqlite_trigger_names()

    assert len(INVARIANT_CATALOG) == 117
    assert set(INVARIANT_CATALOG) == sqlite_triggers
    assert Counter(
        contract.owner for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
    ) == {
        "database_trigger": 48,
        "orm_guard": 69,
    }
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_workflow_foreach_items_identity_frozen"
    ].owner == "database_trigger"
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_workflow_foreach_items_no_delete"
    ].owner == "database_trigger"
    assert all(contract.rationale.strip() for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values())
    database_contracts = {
        name: contract
        for name, contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.items()
        if contract.owner == "database_trigger"
    }
    assert all(contract.table_name for contract in database_contracts.values())
    assert all(
        contract.event in {"UPDATE", "DELETE"}
        for contract in database_contracts.values()
    )
    assert all(contract.message for contract in database_contracts.values())
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen"
    ].owner == "database_trigger"
    assert INVARIANT_CATALOG[
        "trg_evaluation_packages_state_transition"
    ].owner == "orm_guard"

    assert len(MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS) == 8


def test_mysql_partial_unique_guards_install_as_generated_unique_indexes(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)
        generated = {
            (str(row.TABLE_NAME), str(row.COLUMN_NAME)): (
                str(row.GENERATION_EXPRESSION),
                str(row.EXTRA),
            )
            for row in connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT TABLE_NAME, COLUMN_NAME, GENERATION_EXPRESSION, EXTRA "
                    "FROM information_schema.COLUMNS "
                    "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() "
                    "AND COLUMN_NAME LIKE 'g_uq_%'"
                )
            )
        }
        indexes = {
            str(row.INDEX_NAME): (
                str(row.TABLE_NAME),
                tuple(
                    str(column.COLUMN_NAME)
                    for column in connection.execute(
                        text(
                            "SELECT COLUMN_NAME FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
                            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() "
                            "AND TABLE_NAME = :table_name "
                            "AND INDEX_NAME = :index_name "
                            "ORDER BY SEQ_IN_INDEX"
                        ),
                        {
                            "table_name": str(row.TABLE_NAME),
                            "index_name": str(row.INDEX_NAME),
                        },
                    )
                ),
            )
            for row in connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT DISTINCT TABLE_NAME, INDEX_NAME "
                    "FROM information_schema.STATISTICS "
                    "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND NON_UNIQUE = 0 "
                    "AND INDEX_NAME LIKE 'uq_%'"
                )
            )
        }

    for spec in MYSQL_PARTIAL_UNIQUE_SPECS:
        expression, extra = generated[(spec.table_name, spec.guard_column)]
        assert "case" in expression.lower()
        assert "virtual generated" in extra.lower()
        assert indexes[spec.index_name] == (
            spec.table_name,
            (*spec.key_columns, spec.guard_column),
        )

def test_mysql_partial_unique_guards_serialize_concurrent_matching_writers(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)

    races = (
        (
            "category_evaluation_v3_revisions",
            (
                {
                    "category_key": "race-category",
                    "revision": 100,
                    "contract_hash": "1" * 64,
                },
                {
                    "category_key": "race-category",
                    "revision": 101,
                    "contract_hash": "2" * 64,
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "evaluation_jobs",
            (
                {
                    "asset_id": 10101,
                    "regression_item_id": 10111,
                    "strategy_bundle_id": 10121,
                },
                {
                    "asset_id": 10102,
                    "regression_item_id": 10111,
                    "strategy_bundle_id": 10121,
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "evaluation_jobs",
            (
                {
                    "asset_id": 10201,
                    "regression_item_id": None,
                    "strategy_bundle_id": None,
                    "baseline_regression_item_id": 10211,
                },
                {
                    "asset_id": 10202,
                    "regression_item_id": None,
                    "strategy_bundle_id": None,
                    "baseline_regression_item_id": 10211,
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "source_identity_verifications",
            (
                {
                    "contract_key": "race-source-contract",
                    "source_system": "race-source",
                    "probe_hash": "3" * 64,
                },
                {
                    "contract_key": "race-source-contract",
                    "source_system": "race-source",
                    "probe_hash": "4" * 64,
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "content_records",
            (
                {
                    "source_content_id": "race-content-1",
                    "content_key": "race-content-key",
                },
                {
                    "source_content_id": "race-content-2",
                    "content_key": "race-content-key",
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "tag_demand_contracts",
            (
                {
                    "contract_key": "race-tag-contract",
                    "version": 100,
                    "contract_hash": "5" * 64,
                },
                {
                    "contract_key": "race-tag-contract",
                    "version": 101,
                    "contract_hash": "6" * 64,
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "semantic_tag_facts",
            (
                {
                    "asset_version_id": 10301,
                    "field_key": "race-field",
                    "fact_version": 100,
                    "source_evaluation_id": 10311,
                    "source_review_id": 10321,
                    "contract_id": 10331,
                    "payload_hash": "7" * 64,
                },
                {
                    "asset_version_id": 10301,
                    "field_key": "race-field",
                    "fact_version": 101,
                    "source_evaluation_id": 10311,
                    "source_review_id": 10321,
                    "contract_id": 10331,
                    "payload_hash": "8" * 64,
                },
            ),
        ),
        (
            "baseline_truth_revisions",
            (
                {
                    "baseline_set_item_id": 10401,
                    "source_correction_key": "race-correction-key",
                    "canonical_hash": "9" * 64,
                },
                {
                    "baseline_set_item_id": 10402,
                    "source_correction_key": "race-correction-key",
                    "canonical_hash": "a" * 64,
                },
            ),
        ),
    )

    for table_name, competitors in races:
        barrier = threading.Barrier(2)

        def compete(values: dict[str, object]) -> str:
            with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout = 5"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0")
                connection.commit()
                transaction = connection.begin()
                try:
                    barrier.wait(timeout=5)
                    _insert_core_row(connection, table_name, **values)
                    transaction.commit()
                    return "committed"
                except IntegrityError:
                    transaction.rollback()
                    return "rejected"
                finally:
                    connection.exec_driver_sql(
                        "SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1"
                    )
                    connection.commit()

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
            outcomes = list(executor.map(compete, competitors))

        assert Counter(outcomes) == {"committed": 1, "rejected": 1}, table_name


def test_mysql_installed_invariant_inventory_reports_all_database_triggers(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)

        assert installed_mysql_invariants(connection) == {
            name
            for name, contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.items()
            if contract.owner == "database_trigger"
        }


def test_mysql_database_triggers_reject_append_only_mutations_and_deletes(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)
        connection.execute(
            Base.metadata.tables["audit_events"].insert(),
            {
                "event_key": "mysql-invariant-audit",
                "category": "runtime",
                "action": "created",
                "subject_type": "run",
                "subject_id": "1",
                "actor": "original",
                "payload_json": "{}",
            },
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 0")
        _insert_core_row(
            connection,
            "baseline_truth_revisions",
            baseline_set_item_id=20001,
            source_correction_key="trigger-correction-key",
            canonical_hash="1" * 64,
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET SESSION FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS = 1")

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="immutable"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "UPDATE audit_events SET actor='changed' "
                    "WHERE event_key='mysql-invariant-audit'"
                )
            )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="cannot be deleted"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "DELETE FROM audit_events "
                    "WHERE event_key='mysql-invariant-audit'"
                )
            )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="truth revision is immutable"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "UPDATE baseline_truth_revisions SET reason='changed' "
                    "WHERE source_correction_key='trigger-correction-key'"
                )
            )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="truth revision cannot be deleted"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "DELETE FROM baseline_truth_revisions "
                    "WHERE source_correction_key='trigger-correction-key'"
                )
            )

)

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeededNativeCategory:
    category_key: str
    workflow_version_id: int
    baseline_run_id: int
    production_run_id: int
    prompt_version_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    rule_module_version_ids: tuple[int, ...]
    model_config_id: int
    model_parameters: tuple[tuple[str, int, str], ...]
    expected_result: dict[str, Any]
    active_route: str | None = None


def _frozen_component_selection(
    workflow: WorkflowVersion,
) -> tuple[
    tuple[int, ...],
    tuple[int, ...],
    int,
    tuple[tuple[str, int, str], ...],
]:
    nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
    prompt_versions: list[int] = []
    rule_versions: list[int] = []
    model_ids: set[int] = set()
    model_parameters: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
    for node in nodes:
        config = node.get("config") or {}
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
            prompt_versions.append(int(config["prompt_version_id"]))
            model_snapshot = config.get("model_snapshot") or {}
            model_ids.add(int(model_snapshot["source_model_config_id"]))
            model_parameters.append(
                (
                    str(node["node_instance_id"]),
                    int(config["max_tokens"]),
                    str(config["thinking_level"]),
                )
            )
        elif node.get("kind") == "rule_module":
            rule_versions.append(int(config["rule_module_version_id"]))
    assert len(model_ids) == 1
    return (
        tuple(prompt_versions),
        tuple(rule_versions),
        next(iter(model_ids)),
        tuple(model_parameters),
    )


def _ensure_test_categories(db: Session) -> None:
    inspiration = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "inspiration_image"
        )
    )
    if inspiration is None:
        db.add(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                display_name="灵感图",
                status="active",
                allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                pipeline_config_json="{}",
                created_by="system:mysql-native-smoke",
            )
        )
    seed_defaults(db)


def _existing_category_workflow(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
) -> tuple[WorkflowVersion, dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    ensure_native_category_workflows(
        db,
        category_key,
        actor="system:mysql-native-smoke",
    )
    revision = db.scalar(
        select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision)
        .where(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key,
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.status == "active",
        )
        .order_by(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.revision.desc(),
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.id.desc(),
        )
    )
    assert revision is not None
    mapping = db.scalar(
        select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
            LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id == revision.id
        )
    )
    assert mapping is not None
    workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, mapping.workflow_version_id)
    assert workflow is not None
    if category_key == "inspiration_image":
        primary_category, track_key = "建筑设计", "class_one"
    else:
        primary_category, track_key = "家装", "space_building"
    fixture = _fixture(
        revision_bundle(revision),
        primary_category=primary_category,
        track_key=track_key,
    )
    from app.evaluation_v3_pipeline import recompute_qualified_v3
    from app.worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring

    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=fixture["v3_context"],
        precheck=fixture["precheck"],
        dimension_output=fixture["dimension_output"],
    )
    expected = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(
        aggregate,
        precheck=fixture["precheck"],
    )
    asset_snapshot = {
        "workflow_fixture": {
            "schema_version": "native-prompt-fixtures-v1",
            "prompt_outputs": {
                "prompt_a": {"precheck": fixture["precheck"]},
                "prompt_b": {
                    "dimension_output": fixture["dimension_output"]
                },
            },
        }
    }
    return workflow, expected, asset_snapshot


def _knowledge_graph_workflow(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[WorkflowVersion, dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture

    workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
        db,
        actor="system:mysql-native-smoke",
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
    )
    expected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
        "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
        "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
        expected_asset_id="ll-mysql-native-smoke",
    )
    return (
        workflow,
        expected,
        {
            "workflow_input": {
                "source": "domestic",
                "is_single": 0,
                "ll_id": "ll-mysql-native-smoke",
                "fixture_key": "kg.domestic.whole.v1",
            }
        },
    )


def seed_native_category_fixture(
    engine: Engine,
    category_key: str,
) -> SeededNativeCategory:
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        _ensure_test_categories(db)
        if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            workflow, expected, asset_snapshot = _knowledge_graph_workflow(db)
            truth_contract_key = "kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1"
            expected_level = None
            active_route = "domestic_whole"
        else:
            workflow, expected, asset_snapshot = _existing_category_workflow(
                db,
                category_key,
            )
            truth_contract_key = "level.v1"
            expected_level = str(expected["level"])
            active_route = None

        asset = Asset(
            original_name=f"{category_key}-mysql-native.jpg",
            stored_name=f"{category_key}-mysql-native.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=100,
            sha256={
                "knowledge_graph": "1" * 64,
                "inspiration_image": "2" * 64,
                "model_3d_su": "3" * 64,
            }[category_key],
            category_key=category_key,
        )
        db.add(asset)
        baseline = BaselineSet(
            category_key=category_key,
            name=f"{category_key} MySQL 原生验证",
            description="MySQL 原生工作流确定性夹具",
            default_expected_level=expected_level or "L1",
            fingerprint={
                "knowledge_graph": "4" * 64,
                "inspiration_image": "5" * 64,
                "model_3d_su": "6" * 64,
            }[category_key],
            created_by="mysql-smoke",
        )
        db.add(baseline)
        db.flush()
        asset_snapshot.update(
            {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
        )
        db.add(
            BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level=expected_level,
                expected_result_json=canonical_json(
                    expected
                    if category_key == "knowledge_graph"
                    else {"level": expected_level}
                ),
                truth_contract_key=truth_contract_key,
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(asset_snapshot),
            )
        )
        db.flush()
        regression = create_native_baseline_run(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            actor="mysql-smoke",
            idempotency_key=f"mysql-native-{category_key}",
        )
        production_run_id = regression.items[0].production_run_id
        assert production_run_id is not None
        selection = _frozen_component_selection(workflow)
        seeded = SeededNativeCategory(
            category_key=category_key,
            workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
            baseline_run_id=regression.id,
            production_run_id=production_run_id,
            prompt_version_ids=selection[0],
            rule_module_version_ids=selection[1],
            model_config_id=selection[2],
            model_parameters=selection[3],
            expected_result=expected,
            active_route=active_route,
        )
        db.commit()
        return seeded


def execute_fixture_native_run(
    engine: Engine,
    seeded: SeededNativeCategory,
) -> int:
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    original_session_local = executor.SessionLocal
    executor.SessionLocal = sessions
    processed = 0
    try:
        while process_runtime_step_once(
            f"mysql-native-{seeded.category_key}-worker"
        ):
            processed += 1
            if processed > 40:
                raise AssertionError("原生工作流未在 40 个步骤内结束")
    finally:
        executor.SessionLocal = original_session_local
    assert processed > 0
    return seeded.production_run_id


def load_native_run_evidence(
    engine: Engine,
    run_id: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        assert run is not None
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        nodes = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
        prompts = [node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt"]
        rules = [node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "rule_module"]
        model_ids = {
            int(node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["source_model_config_id"])
            for node in prompts
        }
        attempts = {
            item.step_key: {
                "status": item.status,
                "error_code": item.last_error_code,
                "output": json.loads(item.output_manifest_json),
            }
            for item in db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                )
            ).all()
        }
        result = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationResult).where(
                EvaluationResult.production_run_id == run_id
            )
        )
        baseline = db.get(
            BaselineRegressionRun,
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["baseline_run_id"],
        )
        return {
            "status": run.status,
            "category_key": run.category_key,
            "workflow_version_id": snapshot["workflow"][
                "workflow_version_id"
            ],
            "prompt_version_ids": tuple(
                int(node["config"]["prompt_version_id"])
                for node in prompts
            ),
            "rule_module_version_ids": tuple(
                int(node["config"]["rule_module_version_id"])
                for node in rules
            ),
            "model_config_id": next(iter(model_ids)),
            "model_parameters": tuple(
                (
                    node["node_instance_id"],
                    int(node["config"]["max_tokens"]),
                    str(node["config"]["thinking_level"]),
                )
                for node in prompts
            ),
            "attempts": attempts,
            "normalized_result": (
                json.loads(result.normalized_result_json)
                if result is not None
                else None
            ),
            "baseline_status": baseline.status if baseline is not None else None,
        }

    mysql_engine: Engine,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:

        monkeypatch=monkeypatch,
        identity_suffix="mysql",
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "category_key",
    ["knowledge_graph", "inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"],
)
def test_native_workflow_freezes_and_executes_selected_component_versions(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    category_key: str,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    seeded = seed_native_category_fixture(mysql_engine, category_key)

    run_id = execute_fixture_native_run(mysql_engine, seeded)
    evidence = load_native_run_evidence(mysql_engine, run_id)

    assert evidence["status"] == "succeeded"
    assert evidence["baseline_status"] == "completed"
    assert evidence["workflow_version_id"] == seeded.workflow_version_id
    assert evidence["prompt_version_ids"] == seeded.prompt_version_ids
    assert evidence["rule_module_version_ids"] == (
        seeded.rule_module_version_ids
    )
    assert evidence["model_config_id"] == seeded.model_config_id
    assert evidence["model_parameters"] == seeded.model_parameters

    attempts = evidence["attempts"]
    if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
        assert seeded.active_route is not None
        assert attempts[f"prompt_{seeded.active_route}"]["status"] == (
            "succeeded"
        )
        assert attempts[f"project_{seeded.active_route}"]["status"] == (
            "succeeded"
        )
        for route in (
            "domestic_whole",
            "domestic_single",
            "overseas_whole",
            "overseas_single",
        ):
            if route == seeded.active_route:
                continue
            assert attempts[f"prompt_{route}"]["status"] == "skipped"
            assert attempts[f"project_{route}"]["status"] == "skipped"
            assert attempts[f"prompt_{route}"]["error_code"] == (
                "route_not_selected"
            )
            assert attempts[f"project_{route}"]["error_code"] == (
                "route_not_selected"
            )
        assert evidence["normalized_result"] == seeded.expected_result
    else:
        assert attempts["prompt_a"]["status"] == "succeeded"
        assert attempts["prompt_b"]["status"] == "succeeded"
        assert len(seeded.rule_module_version_ids) == 7
        for node_id in (
            "eligibility_redline_1",
            "track_routing_2",
            "media_adjustment_3",
            "quality_cap_exception_4",
            "dimension_scoring_5",
            "anchor_calibration_6",
            "score_level_matcher_7",
        ):
            assert attempts[node_id]["status"] == "succeeded"
        assert evidence["normalized_result"]["level"] == (
            seeded.expected_result["level"]
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "category_key",
    ["knowledge_graph", "inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"],
)
def test_mysql_native_correction_analysis_recommendation_rerun_loop(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    category_key: str,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        append_node_correction_and_replay,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    seeded = seed_native_category_fixture(mysql_engine, category_key)
    execute_fixture_native_run(mysql_engine, seeded)

    with Session(mysql_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        source = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, seeded.baseline_run_id)
        assert source is not None and source.status == "completed"
        source_item = source.items[0]
        if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            corrected_node = "prompt_domestic_whole"
            corrected_field = "style"
            corrected_value: Any = "现代简约,侘寂风"
        else:
            corrected_node = "prompt_b"
            corrected_field = "dimension_output.aesthetic_score"
            corrected_value = 55
        replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
            db,
            run_id=seeded.production_run_id,
            node_instance_id=corrected_node,
            field_path=corrected_field,
            value=corrected_value,
            reason="人工核验后修正节点结果",
            actor="运营验收",
            correction_key=f"mysql-{category_key}-correction",
            evidence=[{"text": "MySQL 原生闭环人工证据"}],
        )
        assert corrected_node in replay.affected_nodes
        if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            assert replay.truth_revision == 1
            assert replay.effective_next_run is True

        correction = BaselineCorrectionRun(
            idempotency_key=f"mysql-{category_key}-candidate-loop",
            baseline_run_id=source.id,
            category_key=category_key,
            selected_item_ids_json=canonical_json([source_item.id]),
            input_snapshot_json="{}",
            created_by="mysql-smoke",
        )
        db.add(correction)
        db.flush()
        analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
            db,
            source.id,
            [source_item.id],
        )
        assert analysis.blockers == ()
        recommendations = recommend_component_versions(db, analysis)
        assert recommendations
        assert {item.node_instance_id for item in recommendations} == {
            corrected_node
        }
        candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
            db,
            seeded.workflow_version_id,
            recommendations,
            correction.id,
            "mysql-smoke",
        )
        assert candidate.id != seeded.workflow_version_id
        candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
            db,
            correction,
            "mysql-smoke",
        )
        assert len(candidate_run.items) == 1
        candidate_production_id = candidate_run.items[0].production_run_id
        assert candidate_production_id is not None
        correction_id = correction.id
        candidate_run_id = candidate_run.id
        candidate_workflow_id = candidate.id
        db.commit()

    execute_fixture_native_run(
        mysql_engine,
        replace(
            seeded,
            workflow_version_id=candidate_workflow_id,
            baseline_run_id=candidate_run_id,
            production_run_id=candidate_production_id,
        ),
    )

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
        candidate_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, candidate_run_id)
        assert correction is not None and candidate_run is not None
        assert candidate_run.status == "completed"
        refresh_correction_run(db, correction)
        assert correction.status == "awaiting_decision"
        assert correction.stage == "decision"
        report = json.loads(correction.report_json)["candidate_regression"]
        assert report["run_id"] == candidate_run.id
        assert isinstance(report["approval_allowed"], bool)

from __future__ import annotations

def _integration_script() -> Any:
    script = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "mysql-integration.py"
    specification = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "labelsystem_mysql_reconciliation_script",
        script,
    )
    assert specification is not None
    assert specification.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(specification)
    specification.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def _workflow_source(
    path: Path,
    *,
    legacy_v81: bool = False,
    legacy_v82: bool = False,
    legacy_v89: bool = False,
) -> Path:
    assert sum((legacy_v81, legacy_v82, legacy_v89)) <= 1
    source = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    now = datetime(2026, 8, 28, 6, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(source)
        with source.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                Base.metadata.tables["workflow_definitions"].insert(),
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "workflow_key": "mysql-reconcile-fixture",
                    "name": "MySQL 对账夹具",
                    "description": "",
                    "owner": "test",
                    "allowed_categories_json": '["knowledge_graph"]',
                    "status": "active",
                    "created_by": "test",
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            connection.execute(
                Base.metadata.tables["workflow_versions"].insert(),
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "workflow_definition_id": 1,
                    "version": "native-v1",
                    "status": "active",
                    "workflow_schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "step_manifest_json": '[{"b":2,"a":1}]',
                    "edge_manifest_json": "[]",
                    "input_schema_json": "{}",
                    "output_schema_json": "{}",
                    "resource_policy_json": "{}",
                    "canonical_hash": "0" * 64,
                    "validation_report_json": "{}",
                    "created_by": "test",
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            if not legacy_v81:
                connection.execute(
                    Base.metadata.tables["workflow_snapshot_blobs"].insert(),
                    {
                        "id": 1,
                        "snapshot_hash": "1" * 64,
                        "canonical_size_bytes": 2,
                        "compressed_size_bytes": 2,
                        "compression": "gzip",
                        "payload_bytes": b"\x00\xff",
                        "created_at": now,
                    },
                )
            if legacy_v81 or legacy_v82 or legacy_v89:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "CREATE TABLE schema_migrations ("
                    "version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL)"
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations(version, name) "
                    "VALUES (?, ?)",
                    (
                        81 if legacy_v81 else 82 if legacy_v82 else 89,
                        (
                            "add_baseline_truth_revisions"
                            if legacy_v81
                            else (
                                "add_runtime_foundation"
                                if legacy_v82
                                else "scope_nas_source_uri_to_category"
                            )
                        ),
                    ),
                )
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "DROP TABLE workflow_model_daily_budgets"
                )
                for table_name in (
                    "paid_acceptance_call_reservations",
                    "paid_acceptance_campaigns",
                    "workflow_external_waits",
                    "workflow_model_media_usage",
                    "human_review_overflow_facts",
                    "human_review_daily_batch_items",
                    "human_review_daily_batches",
                    "human_review_quota_policies",
                ):
                    if not legacy_v89:
                        connection.exec_driver_sql(f"DROP TABLE {table_name}")
                if legacy_v89:
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE image_first_seen_cache")
                else:
                    if legacy_v81:
                        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE runtime_outbox_events")
                        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE workflow_snapshot_blobs")
                    else:
                        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE runtime_outbox_events")
                        connection.exec_driver_sql(
                            """
                        CREATE TABLE runtime_outbox_events (
                            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                            event_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                            target_contract_key VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
                            business_idempotency_key VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
                            mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                                REFERENCES workflow_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                            payload_blob_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                                REFERENCES workflow_snapshot_blobs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                            payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
                            status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
                            available_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                            attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                            max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 5,
                            lease_owner VARCHAR(120),
                            lease_token VARCHAR(80),
                            leased_at DATETIME,
                            heartbeat_at DATETIME,
                            lease_expires_at DATETIME,
                            last_error_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                            last_error_message TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                            delivery_receipt_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                            delivered_at DATETIME,
                            dead_lettered_at DATETIME,
                            created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                            updated_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                            UNIQUE(target_contract_key, business_idempotency_key)
                        )
                            """
                        )
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE upstream_intake_events")
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE connector_pause_states")
                    connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE connector_contract_versions")
    finally:
        source.dispose()
    path.chmod(0o400)
    return path


def _create_and_import(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
) -> None:
    with target.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
    import_sqlite_snapshot_to_mysql(
        source,
        target,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=20,
    )


def test_binary_values_have_a_deterministic_canonical_form() -> None:
    column = Column("payload_bytes", LargeBinary)

    assert canonical_database_value(b"\x00\xff", column) == b"Y\x00\xff"
    assert canonical_database_value(memoryview(b"\x00\xff"), column) == (
        b"Y\x00\xff"
    )


def test_fractional_value_in_integer_column_is_rejected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="非法整数"):
        canonical_database_value(1.5, Column("item_id", Integer))


def test_float_value_in_boolean_column_is_rejected() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="非法布尔值"):
        canonical_database_value(1.0, Column("enabled", Boolean))


def test_auto_increment_reconciliation_bypasses_cached_mysql_statistics() -> None:
    class CachedStatisticsConnection:
        def __init__(self) -> None:
            self.fresh = False

        def exec_driver_sql(self, statement: str) -> None:
            if statement == "SET SESSION information_schema_stats_expiry = 0":
                self.fresh = True

        def execute(self, _statement: object) -> list[SimpleNamespace]:
            return [
                SimpleNamespace(
                    TABLE_NAME=table.name,
                    AUTO_INCREMENT=(
                        11 if self.fresh and table.name == "users" else 1
                    ),
                )
                for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
                if table._autoincrement_column is not None
            ]

    connection = CachedStatisticsConnection()
    tables = {
        table.name: SimpleNamespace(
            target_primary_key_max=10 if table.name == "users" else None
        )
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
    }

    states = _auto_increment_states(connection, tables)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    assert states["users"].next_value == 11
    assert states["users"].safe is True


def test_mysql_reconciliation_accepts_every_mapped_fact_and_structure(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _workflow_source(tmp_path / "source.db")
    _create_and_import(source, mysql_engine, tmp_path / "checkpoints")

    report = reconcile_mysql_databases(source, mysql_engine)
    assert_mysql_critical_invariants(report)

    assert set(report.tables) == set(Base.metadata.tables)
    assert len(report.tables) == 121
    assert all(item.matches for item in report.tables.values())
    assert report.target_mysql_version.startswith("8.4.")
    assert report.target_schema_version == max(
        migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS
    )
    assert len(report.installed_invariant_triggers) == 48
    assert len(report.generated_unique_guards) == 8
    assert len(report.generated_guard_indexes) == 8
    assert report.source_uri_hash_contract is True
    assert all(fact["compatible"] for fact in report.schema_facts.values())
    assert all(state.safe for state in report.auto_increments.values())
    assert len(report.data_evidence_sha256) == 64
    rendered_json = json.dumps(
        report.as_dict(),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
    )
    rendered_markdown = report.to_markdown(accepted=True)
    assert "SQLite → MySQL 8.4 对账报告" in rendered_markdown
    assert "mysql+pymysql://" not in rendered_json + rendered_markdown
    assert "must-not-echo" not in rendered_json + rendered_markdown
    missing_guard = replace(
        report,
        generated_unique_guards=report.generated_unique_guards[:-1],
    )
    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="generated_guards"):
        assert_mysql_critical_invariants(missing_guard)

        assert report.tables[table_name].target_row_count == 0
        assert report.tables[table_name].matches is True

def test_mysql_reconciliation_blocks_a_changed_workflow_fact(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _workflow_source(tmp_path / "source.db")
    _create_and_import(source, mysql_engine, tmp_path / "checkpoints")
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM workflow_versions WHERE id=1"))

    report = reconcile_mysql_databases(source, mysql_engine)

    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="workflow_versions"):
        assert_mysql_critical_invariants(report)


def test_mysql_reconciliation_cli_writes_private_safe_receipts(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    module = _integration_script()
    source = tmp_path / "frozen.db"
    source.write_bytes(b"sqlite-fixture")
    accounts = tmp_path / "accounts.json"
    accounts.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    json_report = tmp_path / "reports" / "reconciliation.json"
    markdown_report = tmp_path / "reports" / "reconciliation.md"
    calls: dict[str, object] = {}

    class FakeConnection:
        def __enter__(self) -> "FakeConnection":
            return self

        def __exit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            return None

    class FakeEngine:
        def connect(self) -> FakeConnection:
            return FakeConnection()

        def dispose(self) -> None:
            calls["disposed"] = True

    fake_engine = FakeEngine()
    fake_report = SimpleNamespace(
        as_dict=lambda: {
            "source": str(source.resolve()),
            "target_schema": "labels_migration",
            "tables": {"users": {"matches": True}},
        },
        to_markdown=lambda *, accepted, error="": (
            "# SQLite → MySQL 8.4 对账报告\n\n"
            f"- 结论：{'通过' if accepted else '阻断'}\n"
            f"{error}\n"
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "mysql_database_url_from_references",
        lambda accounts_file, *, role, database: calls.update(
            {
                "accounts": accounts_file,
                "role": role,
                "database": database,
            }
        )
        or "mysql+pymysql://migration:must-not-echo@localhost/labels",
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "create_mysql_migration_engine",
        lambda url: calls.setdefault("url", url) and fake_engine,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_runtime_contract",
        lambda connection: calls.setdefault("contract", connection),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "reconcile_mysql_databases",
        lambda source_path, target: calls.update(
            {"source": source_path, "target": target}
        )
        or fake_report,
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_critical_invariants",
        lambda report: calls.setdefault("asserted", report),
        raising=False,
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "reconcile-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts),
            "--json-report",
            str(json_report),
            "--markdown-report",
            str(markdown_report),
        ],
        output=output,
    )

    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    receipt = json.loads(json_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert exit_code == 0
    assert calls["role"] == "migration"
    assert calls["database"] == "labels_migration"
    assert calls["source"] == source
    assert calls["target"] is fake_engine
    assert calls["asserted"] is fake_report
    assert calls["disposed"] is True
    assert receipt["accepted"] is True
    assert receipt["error"] == ""
    assert receipt["report"]["target_schema"] == "labels_migration"
    assert "结论：通过" in markdown_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert stat.S_IMODE(json_report.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
    assert stat.S_IMODE(markdown_report.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
    assert payload == {
        "accepted": True,
        "command": "reconcile-snapshot",
        "json_report": str(json_report.resolve()),
        "markdown_report": str(markdown_report.resolve()),
        "status": "ok",
        "target_schema": "labels_migration",
    }
    rendered = output.getvalue() + json_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    rendered += markdown_report.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "must-not-echo" not in rendered

    def block_reconciliation(_report: object) -> None:
        raise ReconciliationError("tables=workflow_versions")

    blocked_json = tmp_path / "reports" / "blocked.json"
    blocked_markdown = tmp_path / "reports" / "blocked.md"
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module,
        "assert_mysql_critical_invariants",
        block_reconciliation,
    )
    blocked_output = StringIO()

    blocked_exit_code = module.main(
        [
            "reconcile-snapshot",
            "--source",
            str(source),
            "--database",
            "labels_migration",
            "--accounts",
            str(accounts),
            "--json-report",
            str(blocked_json),
            "--markdown-report",
            str(blocked_markdown),
        ],
        output=blocked_output,
    )

    blocked_receipt = json.loads(blocked_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert blocked_exit_code == 2
    assert json.loads(blocked_output.getvalue())["status"] == "blocked"
    assert blocked_receipt["accepted"] is False
    assert blocked_receipt["error"] == "tables=workflow_versions"
    assert "结论：阻断" in blocked_markdown.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

from __future__ import annotations

import re
