    CheckConstraint,
    DateTime,
    Text,
    UniqueConstraint,
    inspect,
    text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine
from sqlalchemy.exc import DBAPIError, IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateTable

from app import models  # noqa: F401
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import (
    EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES,
    MySQLBaselineError,
    assert_mysql_schema_version,
    create_mysql_baseline,
    mysql_metadata,
    mysql_schema_fingerprint,
    upgrade_mysql_schema,
)
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION = max(migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS)
MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS = {
    "fidelity_mode",
    "fidelity_value",
    "preprocess_mode",
    "preprocess_value",
    "policy_hash",
    "capability_profile_key",
    "normalized_pixel_hash",
    "compression_ms",
    "saved_bytes",
    "saved_ratio_ppm",
    "provider_max_bytes",
    "operational_max_bytes",
    "size_gate_result",
    "estimation_formula_version",
}


def _compiled_tables() -> str:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    return "\n".join(
        str(CreateTable(table).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
    ).lower()


def _check_sql(table_name: str, constraint_name: str) -> str:
    table = mysql_metadata().tables[table_name]
    for constraint in table.constraints:
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint) and constraint.name == constraint_name:
            return str(constraint.sqltext).lower()
    raise AssertionError(f"缺少约束 {table_name}.{constraint_name}")


def test_mysql_metadata_compiles_every_table_without_sqlite_fragments() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    ddl = _compiled_tables()

    assert len(metadata.tables) == 153
    assert {
        "connector_contract_versions",
        "upstream_intake_events",
        "connector_pause_states",
        "model_quota_pools",
        "model_quota_leases",
        "model_quota_minute_windows",
        "image_first_seen_cache",
        "workflow_foreach_items",
    } <= set(metadata.tables)
    assert " glob " not in ddl
    assert "json_type(definition_json, '$')" not in ddl
    assert "json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level') = expected_level" not in ddl
    assert (
        "json_unquote(json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level')) = expected_level"
        in ddl
    )
    assert "regexp_like(" in ddl


def test_mysql_metadata_carries_foreach_and_keyed_invocation_identity() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    foreach = metadata.tables["workflow_foreach_items"]
    invocations = metadata.tables["workflow_model_invocations"]

    assert {
        "production_run_id",
        "parent_step_attempt_id",
        "item_key",
        "input_hash",
        "output_hash",
    } <= set(foreach.c.keys())
    assert "invocation_key" in invocations.c
    unique_columns = {
        tuple(column.name for column in constraint.columns)
        for constraint in invocations.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, UniqueConstraint)
    }
    assert ("step_attempt_id", "invocation_key") in unique_columns
    assert ("step_attempt_id",) not in unique_columns


def test_mysql_metadata_translates_json_root_kinds_and_scalar_strings() -> None:
    object_check = _check_sql(
        "runtime_audit_events",
        "ck_runtime_audit_events_details_json",
    )
    array_check = _check_sql(
        "evaluation_production_runs",
        "ck_evaluation_production_runs_blockers_json",
    )
    level_check = _check_sql(
        "baseline_truth_revisions",
        "ck_baseline_truth_revisions_shape",
    )

    assert "lower(json_type(json_extract(details_json, '$'))) = 'object'" in object_check
    assert "lower(json_type(json_extract(blockers_json, '$'))) = 'array'" in array_check
    assert (
        "lower(json_type(json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level'))) = 'string'"
        in level_check
    )
    assert (
        "json_unquote(json_extract(expected_result_json, '$.level')) = expected_level"
        in level_check
    )

def test_mysql_metadata_removes_all_nine_unsupported_partial_indexes() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    names = {
        index.name
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for index in table.indexes
    }
    source_names = {
        index.name
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for index in table.indexes
        if index.dialect_options["sqlite"].get("where") is not None
    }

    assert len(EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES) == 9
    assert not names.intersection(EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES)
    assert source_names == EXPECTED_PARTIAL_INDEX_NAMES


def test_mysql_metadata_defers_auto_increment_checks_to_triggers() -> None:
    trigger_backed = {
        "ck_dimension_schemas_parent_not_self",
        "ck_dimension_schemas_core_not_self",
    }
    native_singletons = {
        "ck_queue_scheduler_state_singleton",
        "ck_automation_policy_singleton",
    }
    mysql_checks = {
        constraint.name
        for table in mysql_metadata().tables.values()
        for constraint in table.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
    }
    canonical_checks = {
        constraint.name
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for constraint in table.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
    }

    assert trigger_backed | native_singletons <= canonical_checks
    assert not trigger_backed.intersection(mysql_checks)
    assert native_singletons <= mysql_checks


def test_mysql_metadata_preserves_microseconds_and_case_sensitive_text() -> None:
    expected_datetime_columns = sum(
        isinstance(column.type, DateTime)
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
    )
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    datetime_columns = [
        column
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, mysql.DATETIME)
    ]

    assert len(datetime_columns) == expected_datetime_columns
    assert all(column.type.fsp == 6 for column in datetime_columns)
    for table_name, column_name in (
        ("workflow_definitions", "workflow_key"),
        ("runtime_outbox_events", "event_key"),
        ("content_records", "content_key"),
    ):
        assert (
            metadata.tables[table_name].c[column_name].type.collation
            == "utf8mb4_0900_as_cs"
        )

def test_mysql_metadata_matches_current_timestamp_to_datetime_precision() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()

    for table_name in ("dimension_schemas", "dimension_route_policies"):
        created_at = metadata.tables[table_name].c.created_at
        ddl = str(
            CreateTable(metadata.tables[table_name]).compile(
                dialect=mysql.dialect()
            )
        ).lower()

        assert isinstance(created_at.type, mysql.DATETIME)
        assert created_at.type.fsp == 6
        assert str(created_at.server_default.arg).lower() == "current_timestamp(6)"
        assert "created_at datetime(6) not null default current_timestamp(6)" in ddl


def test_mysql_metadata_does_not_mutate_canonical_datetime_types() -> None:
    expected_datetime_columns = {
        (table.name, column.name)
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, DateTime)
    }
    mysql_metadata()

    canonical_datetime_columns = {
        (table.name, column.name): column
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, DateTime)
    }

    assert set(canonical_datetime_columns) == expected_datetime_columns
    assert all(
        not isinstance(column.type, mysql.DATETIME)
        for column in canonical_datetime_columns.values()
    )


def test_mysql_metadata_wraps_every_text_default_as_an_expression() -> None:
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    text_defaults = [
        str(column.server_default.arg)
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for column in table.columns
        if isinstance(column.type, mysql.LONGTEXT)
        and column.server_default is not None
    ]
    users_ddl = str(
        CreateTable(metadata.tables["users"]).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect())
    ).lower()

    assert len(text_defaults) == 84
    assert all(default.startswith("(") and default.endswith(")") for default in text_defaults)
    assert "permissions_json longtext" in users_ddl
    assert "default ('[]')" in users_ddl


def test_mysql_metadata_preserves_category_scoped_long_source_uri_digest() -> None:
    assets = mysql_metadata().tables["assets"]
    source_unique_constraints = [
        constraint
        for constraint in assets.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, UniqueConstraint)
        and [column.name for column in constraint.columns] == ["source_uri"]
    ]
    digest_unique_constraints = [
        constraint
        for constraint in assets.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, UniqueConstraint)
        and constraint.name == "uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category"
    ]

    assert assets.c.source_uri.type.length == 1000
    assert assets.c.source_uri.type.collation == "utf8mb4_0900_bin"
    assert source_unique_constraints == []
    assert len(digest_unique_constraints) == 1
    assert [
        column.name for column in digest_unique_constraints[0].columns
    ] == ["source_uri_sha256", "category_key"]
    assert str(assets.c.source_uri_sha256.type).lower() == "binary(32)"
    assert assets.c.source_uri_sha256.computed is not None
    assert (
        str(assets.c.source_uri_sha256.computed.sqltext).lower()
        == "unhex(sha2(source_uri, 256))"
    )


def test_mysql_baseline_creates_every_table_and_records_latest_migration(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

    actual = set(inspect(mysql_engine).get_table_names())
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        recorded = connection.execute(
            text("SELECT version, name FROM schema_migrations ORDER BY version")
        ).all()

    assert set(Base.metadata.tables) <= actual
    assert "schema_migrations" in actual
    assert recorded == [(migration.version, migration.name) for migration in MIGRATIONS]
    assert recorded[-1][0] == LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION


def test_mysql_baseline_refuses_a_nonempty_target_schema(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE existing_fact (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)")

    with pytest.raises(MySQLBaselineError, match="空 schema"):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

    assert "existing_fact" in inspect(mysql_engine).get_table_names()

def test_mysql_baseline_rejects_dimension_schema_self_reference_on_insert(

) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

                    "(9001, 'self-parent', 'v1', 'extension', 'common', "
                    "'Self parent', 'draft', 9001, '{}', :canonical_hash, 'test')"
                ),
                {"canonical_hash": "a" * 64},
            )

def test_mysql_baseline_enforces_category_scoped_uniqueness_for_long_source_uri(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)

    source_uri = "https://example.test/assets/" + "界" * 850
    insert = text(
        "INSERT INTO assets "
        "(original_name, stored_name, source_uri, mime_type, size_bytes, sha256, "
        "category_key, status, created_at) VALUES "
        "(:original_name, :stored_name, :source_uri, 'image/jpeg', 1, :sha256, "
        ":category_key, 'ready', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))"
    )
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(
            insert,
            {
                "original_name": "first.jpg",
                "stored_name": "first.jpg",
                "source_uri": source_uri,
                "sha256": "1" * 64,
                "category_key": "space_image",
            },
        )
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT CHAR_LENGTH(source_uri) FROM assets"
        ).scalar_one() == len(source_uri)

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(
            insert,
            {
                "original_name": "second.jpg",
                "stored_name": "second.jpg",
                "source_uri": source_uri,
                "sha256": "2" * 64,
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
            },
        )

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                insert,
                {
                    "original_name": "third.jpg",
                    "stored_name": "third.jpg",
                    "source_uri": source_uri,
                    "sha256": "3" * 64,
                    "category_key": "space_image",
                },
            )


def test_mysql_schema_89_upgrades_global_source_uri_digest_to_category_scope(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `assets` DROP INDEX "
            "`uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX `uq_assets_source_uri_sha256` "
            "ON `assets` (`source_uri_sha256`)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= %s",
            (89,),
        )
        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=89)

        indexes = {
            item["name"]: tuple(item["column_names"])
            for item in inspect(connection).get_indexes("assets")
        }
        assert "uq_assets_source_uri_sha256" not in indexes
        assert indexes["uq_assets_source_uri_sha256_category"] == (
            "source_uri_sha256",
            "category_key",
        )
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 89


def test_mysql_schema_95_upgrades_foreach_and_invocation_identity(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE `workflow_foreach_items`")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` DROP INDEX "
            "`uq_workflow_model_invocations_attempt_key`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` DROP COLUMN "
            "`invocation_key`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` ADD CONSTRAINT "
            "`uq_workflow_model_invocations_step_attempt` "
            "UNIQUE (`step_attempt_id`)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= %s",
            (95,),
        )

        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=95)

        inspector = inspect(connection)
        assert "workflow_foreach_items" in inspector.get_table_names()
        assert "invocation_key" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("workflow_model_invocations")
        }
        unique_constraints = {
            item["name"]: tuple(item["column_names"])
            for item in inspector.get_unique_constraints(
                "workflow_model_invocations"
            )
        }
        assert unique_constraints[
            "uq_workflow_model_invocations_attempt_key"
        ] == ("step_attempt_id", "invocation_key")
        assert "uq_workflow_model_invocations_step_attempt" not in unique_constraints
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 95


def test_mysql_schema_96_upgrades_frozen_tier_pricing_evidence(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        for table_name, constraint_names in (
            ("model_configs", ("ck_model_configs_pricing_contract",)),
            (
                "model_registry_entries",
                ("ck_model_registry_pricing_contract",),
            ),
            (
                "workflow_model_invocations",
                (
                    "ck_workflow_model_invocations_pricing_snapshot",
                    "ck_workflow_model_invocations_actual_cost",
                ),
            ),
        ):
            for constraint_name in constraint_names:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    f"ALTER TABLE `{table_name}` DROP CHECK `{constraint_name}`"
                )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `model_configs` DROP COLUMN `pricing_contract_json`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE `model_registry_entries` "
            "DROP COLUMN `pricing_contract_json`"
        )
        for column_name in (
            "pricing_snapshot_json",
            "pricing_tier_key",
            "actual_cost_micros",
        ):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "ALTER TABLE `workflow_model_invocations` "
                f"DROP COLUMN `{column_name}`"
            )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= %s",
            (96,),
        )

        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=96)

        assert "pricing_contract_json" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(connection).get_columns("model_configs")
        }
        assert "pricing_contract_json" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                "model_registry_entries"
            )
        }
        assert {
            "pricing_snapshot_json",
            "pricing_tier_key",
            "actual_cost_micros",
        } <= {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspect(connection).get_columns(
                "workflow_model_invocations"
            )
        }
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 96
        trigger_sql = connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT ACTION_STATEMENT FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
            "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TRIGGER_NAME = %s",
            ("trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen",),
        ).scalar_one()
        assert "pricing_snapshot_json" in str(trigger_sql)

        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=0")
        try:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations ("
                "id, production_run_id, step_attempt_id, invocation_key, "
                "node_instance_id, status, provider, model_id, "
                "pricing_snapshot_json, pricing_tier_key, "
                "actual_cost_micros, evidence_json, created_at) VALUES ("
                "900096, 900096, 900096, 'root', 'prompt-a', 'reserved', "
                "'volcengine', 'doubao-seed', "
                "'{\"schema_version\":\"model_pricing_contract_v2\"}', "
                "'', 0, '{}', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
            )
            with pytest.raises(DBAPIError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                    "pricing_snapshot_json='{\"schema_version\":\"tampered\"}' "
                    "WHERE id=900096"
                )

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                "status='succeeded', pricing_tier_key='input_0_32k', "
                "actual_cost_micros=1234 WHERE id=900096"
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT status, pricing_tier_key, actual_cost_micros "
                "FROM workflow_model_invocations WHERE id=900096"
            ).one() == ("succeeded", "input_0_32k", 1234)
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=1")


def test_mysql_schema_97_repairs_legacy_invocation_identity_trigger(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = %s",
            (97,),
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS `trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen`"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TRIGGER `trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen` "
            "BEFORE UPDATE ON `workflow_model_invocations` FOR EACH ROW "
            "BEGIN IF NOT ("
            "OLD.`pricing_snapshot_json` <=> NEW.`pricing_snapshot_json` AND "
            "OLD.`pricing_tier_key` <=> NEW.`pricing_tier_key`"
            ") THEN SIGNAL SQLSTATE '45000' SET MESSAGE_TEXT = "
            "'Workflow model invocation identity is immutable'; END IF; END"
        )

        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=97)

        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 97
        trigger_sql = str(
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT ACTION_STATEMENT FROM information_schema.TRIGGERS "
                "WHERE TRIGGER_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TRIGGER_NAME = %s",
                ("trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen",),
            ).scalar_one()
        )
        assert "pricing_snapshot_json" in trigger_sql
        assert "pricing_tier_key" not in trigger_sql

        connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=0")
        try:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations ("
                "id, production_run_id, step_attempt_id, invocation_key, "
                "node_instance_id, status, provider, model_id, "
                "pricing_snapshot_json, pricing_tier_key, "
                "actual_cost_micros, evidence_json, created_at) VALUES ("
                "900097, 900097, 900097, 'root', 'prompt-a', 'reserved', "
                "'volcengine', 'doubao-seed', "
                "'{\"schema_version\":\"model_pricing_contract_v2\"}', "
                "'', 0, '{}', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                "status='succeeded', pricing_tier_key='input_0_32k', "
                "actual_cost_micros=1234 WHERE id=900097"
            )
            with pytest.raises(DBAPIError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_model_invocations SET "
                    "pricing_snapshot_json='{\"schema_version\":\"tampered\"}' "
                    "WHERE id=900097"
                )
        finally:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("SET FOREIGN_KEY_CHECKS=1")


def test_mysql_schema_fingerprint_is_stable_and_version_guard_fails_closed(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        first = mysql_schema_fingerprint(connection)
        second = mysql_schema_fingerprint(connection)
        assert_mysql_schema_version(connection, expected=LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION)
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = %s",
            (LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION,),
        )

    assert re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", first)
    assert second == first
    with mysql_engine.connect() as connection:
        with pytest.raises(
            MySQLBaselineError,
            match=rf"期望 {LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION}",
        ):
            assert_mysql_schema_version(
                connection,
                expected=LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_mysql_schema_clone.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import stat
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import URL

from app.mysql_schema_clone import (
    CandidateDatabaseAccounts,
    MySQLSchemaCloneError,
    MySQLSchemaEvidence,
    MySQLTableEvidence,
    assert_exact_schema_clone,
    candidate_identity_names,
    clone_schema_with_clients,
    mysql_schema_evidence,
    prepare_candidate_database,
    write_candidate_runtime_dsns,
)


def _write_executable(path: Path, source: str) -> Path:
    path.write_text(source, encoding="utf-8")
    path.chmod(path.stat().st_mode | stat.S_IXUSR)
    return path


def test_candidate_runtime_dsns_stay_private_and_off_external_volumes(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    accounts = CandidateDatabaseAccounts(
        app_username="ls_app_1234567890ab",
        app_password="fixture-app-secret",
        migration_username="ls_mig_1234567890ab",
        migration_password="fixture-migration-secret",
    )
    private_root = tmp_path / "runtime-secrets"

    files = write_candidate_runtime_dsns(
        private_root,
        schema="labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
        host="host.docker.internal",
        port=33084,
        accounts=accounts,
    )

    assert private_root.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o700
    assert files.app.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    assert files.migration.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
    app_url = URL.create("sqlite")
    app_url = app_url.set(
        drivername="mysql+pymysql",
        username="ls_app_1234567890ab",
        password="fixture-app-secret",
        host="host.docker.internal",
        port=33084,
        database="labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
        query={"charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )
    assert files.app.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip() == app_url.render_as_string(
        hide_password=False
    )
    assert "fixture-app-secret" not in repr(files)
    assert "fixture-migration-secret" not in repr(files)

    with pytest.raises(MySQLSchemaCloneError, match="外置卷"):
        write_candidate_runtime_dsns(
            Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD/forbidden-candidate-secrets"),
            schema="labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298",
            host="host.docker.internal",
            port=33084,
            accounts=accounts,
        )


def test_clone_streams_between_real_client_processes_without_passwords_in_argv(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    trace_path = tmp_path / "trace.jsonl"
    monkeypatch.setenv("MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE", str(trace_path))
    dump_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysqldump",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, stat, sys
option_arg = next(value for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
option_file = pathlib.Path(option_arg.split('=', 1)[1])
payload = option_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
assert option_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
assert 'source-fixture-secret' in payload
assert all('source-fixture-secret' not in value for value in sys.argv)
with pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE']).open('a', encoding='utf-8') as output:
    output.write(json.dumps({'kind': 'dump', 'argv': sys.argv[1:], 'option': str(option_file)}) + '\\n')
sys.stdout.write('/*!50003 CREATE*/ /*!50017 DEFINER=`source-user`@`localhost`*/ /*!50003 TRIGGER `clone_probe_trigger` BEFORE UPDATE ON `clone_probe` FOR EACH ROW SET NEW.id=OLD.id */;;\\n')
sys.stdout.write('CREATE TABLE clone_probe(id INTEGER);\\nINSERT INTO clone_probe VALUES (1);\\n')
""",
    )
    restore_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysql",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, sys
option_arg = next(value for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
option_file = pathlib.Path(option_arg.split('=', 1)[1])
payload = option_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
assert option_file.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600
assert 'target-fixture-secret' in payload
assert all('target-fixture-secret' not in value for value in sys.argv)
stream = sys.stdin.read()
assert 'CREATE TABLE clone_probe' in stream
assert 'clone_probe_trigger' in stream
assert 'DEFINER=' not in stream
with pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE']).open('a', encoding='utf-8') as output:
    output.write(json.dumps({'kind': 'restore', 'argv': sys.argv[1:], 'option': str(option_file)}) + '\\n')
""",
    )
    source_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="source-user",
        password="source-fixture-secret",
        host="localhost",
        port=33084,
        database="source_schema",
        query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/source.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )
    target_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="target-user",
        password="target-fixture-secret",
        host="localhost",
        port=33084,
        database="target_schema",
        query={"unix_socket": "/tmp/target.sock", "charset": "utf8mb4"},
    )

    report = clone_schema_with_clients(
        source_url=source_url,
        target_url=target_url,
        source_schema="source_schema",
        target_schema="target_schema",
        mysqldump=dump_client,
        mysql=restore_client,
    )

    trace = [json.loads(line) for line in trace_path.read_text().splitlines()]
    assert report.source_schema == "source_schema"
    assert report.target_schema == "target_schema"
    assert report.stripped_definers == 1
    assert [entry["kind"] for entry in trace] == ["dump", "restore"]
    assert all(
        "source-fixture-secret" not in json.dumps(entry)
        and "target-fixture-secret" not in json.dumps(entry)
        for entry in trace
    )
    assert all(not Path(entry["option"]).exists() for entry in trace)


def test_clone_failure_is_generic_and_cleans_temporary_option_files(
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    trace_path = tmp_path / "failure-trace.json"
    monkeypatch.setenv("MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE", str(trace_path))
    dump_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysqldump",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, sys
option = next(value.split('=', 1)[1] for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE']).write_text(json.dumps({'dump': option}), encoding='utf-8')
sys.stdout.write('SELECT 1;\\n')
""",
    )
    restore_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysql",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import json, os, pathlib, sys
trace = pathlib.Path(os.environ['MYSQL_CLONE_TEST_TRACE'])
payload = json.loads(trace.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
payload['restore'] = next(value.split('=', 1)[1] for value in sys.argv[1:] if value.startswith('--defaults-extra-file='))
trace.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding='utf-8')
sys.stdin.read()
raise SystemExit(7)
""",
    )
    source_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="source-user",
        password="source-fixture-secret",
        database="source_schema",
    )
    target_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="target-user",
        password="target-fixture-secret",
        database="target_schema",
    )

    with pytest.raises(MySQLSchemaCloneError, match="导入失败") as captured:
        clone_schema_with_clients(
            source_url=source_url,
            target_url=target_url,
            source_schema="source_schema",
            target_schema="target_schema",
            mysqldump=dump_client,
            mysql=restore_client,
        )

    assert "fixture-secret" not in str(captured.value)
    trace = json.loads(trace_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert not Path(trace["dump"]).exists()
    assert not Path(trace["restore"]).exists()


def test_restore_failure_is_not_misreported_as_source_broken_pipe(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    dump_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysqldump",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
chunk = b'x' * 65536
for _index in range(1024):
    sys.stdout.buffer.write(chunk)
    sys.stdout.buffer.flush()
""",
    )
    restore_client = _write_executable(
        tmp_path / "mysql",
        """#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
sys.stderr.write('fixture target rejected import')
raise SystemExit(9)
""",
    )
    source_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="source-user",
        password="source-fixture-secret",
        database="source_schema",
    )
    target_url = URL.create(
        "mysql+pymysql",
        username="target-user",
        password="target-fixture-secret",
        database="target_schema",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        MySQLSchemaCloneError,
        match="目标 schema 导入失败",
    ) as captured:
        clone_schema_with_clients(
            source_url=source_url,
            target_url=target_url,
            source_schema="source_schema",
            target_schema="target_schema",
            mysqldump=dump_client,
            mysql=restore_client,
        )

    assert "fixture target rejected import" in str(captured.value)
    assert "fixture-secret" not in str(captured.value)

def test_prepare_candidate_database_uses_exact_schema_grants_and_bound_passwords() -> None:
    target_schema = "labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298"
    names = candidate_identity_names(target_schema)
    accounts = CandidateDatabaseAccounts(
        app_username=names.app_username,
        app_password="fixture-app-secret",
        migration_username=names.migration_username,
        migration_password="fixture-migration-secret",
    )

    class Result:
        def __init__(self, value: object) -> None:
            self.value = value

        def one_or_none(self) -> object:
            return self.value

        def scalar_one(self) -> object:
            return self.value

    class RecordingConnection:
        dialect = SimpleNamespace(name="mysql")

        def __init__(self) -> None:
            self.calls: list[tuple[str, object]] = []

        def exec_driver_sql(
            self,
            statement: str,
            parameters: object = None,
        ) -> Result:
            if isinstance(parameters, tuple):
                statement % tuple("<bound>" for _value in parameters)
            elif parameters is None:
                statement % {}
            self.calls.append((statement, parameters))
            if "DEFAULT_CHARACTER_SET_NAME" in statement:
                return Result(("utf8mb4", "utf8mb4_0900_bin"))
            if "information_schema.SCHEMATA" in statement:
                return Result(0)
            if "mysql.user" in statement:
                return Result(0)
            return Result(None)

    connection = RecordingConnection()

    prepare_candidate_database(
        connection,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        source_schema="labelsystem_integration_candidate_20260829_f3b95eb",
        target_schema=target_schema,
        accounts=accounts,
    )

    statements = "\n".join(statement for statement, _ in connection.calls)
    parameters = [parameters for _, parameters in connection.calls]
    assert "CREATE DATABASE `labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298`" in statements
    assert "GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE" in statements
    assert "GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES" in statements
    assert "`labelsystem_docker_candidate_20260829_04c8298`.*" in statements
    assert "fixture-app-secret" not in statements
    assert "fixture-migration-secret" not in statements
    assert ("fixture-app-secret",) in parameters
    assert ("fixture-migration-secret",) in parameters


def test_exact_schema_clone_rejects_one_row_checksum_drift() -> None:
    source = MySQLSchemaEvidence(
        schema="source_schema",
        migration=87,
        tables={
            "assets": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=2, checksum=111),
            "workflow_versions": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=3, checksum=222),
        },
        views=("current_labels",),
        trigger_count=0,
    )
    exact_target = MySQLSchemaEvidence(
        schema="target_schema",
        migration=87,
        tables={
            "assets": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=2, checksum=111),
            "workflow_versions": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=3, checksum=222),
        },
        views=("current_labels",),
        trigger_count=0,
    )

    report = assert_exact_schema_clone(source, exact_target)

    assert report.table_count == 2
    assert report.row_count == 5
    assert len(report.evidence_hash) == 64

    drifted_target = MySQLSchemaEvidence(
        schema="target_schema",
        migration=87,
        tables={
            "assets": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=2, checksum=999),
            "workflow_versions": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=3, checksum=222),
        },
        views=("current_labels",),
        trigger_count=0,
    )
    with pytest.raises(MySQLSchemaCloneError, match="assets"):
        assert_exact_schema_clone(source, drifted_target)


def test_mysql_schema_evidence_reads_qualified_counts_and_checksums() -> None:
    class Result:
        def __init__(self, value: object) -> None:
            self.value = value

        def scalar_one(self) -> object:
            return self.value

        def all(self) -> object:
            return self.value

        def one(self) -> object:
            return self.value

    class EvidenceConnection:
        dialect = SimpleNamespace(name="mysql")

        def exec_driver_sql(
            self,
            statement: str,
            parameters: object = None,
        ) -> Result:
            if "MAX(version)" in statement:
                return Result(87)
            if "TABLE_TYPE='BASE TABLE'" in statement:
                return Result([("assets",), ("workflow_versions",)])
            if "TABLE_TYPE='VIEW'" in statement:
                return Result([("current_labels",)])
            if "information_schema.TRIGGERS" in statement:
                return Result(0)
            if "COUNT(*) FROM `source_schema`.`assets`" in statement:
                return Result(2)
            if "COUNT(*) FROM `source_schema`.`workflow_versions`" in statement:
                return Result(3)
            if "CHECKSUM TABLE `source_schema`.`assets`" in statement:
                return Result(("source_schema.assets", 111))
            if "CHECKSUM TABLE `source_schema`.`workflow_versions`" in statement:
                return Result(("source_schema.workflow_versions", 222))
            raise AssertionError((statement, parameters))

    evidence = mysql_schema_evidence(
        EvidenceConnection(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        "source_schema",
    )

    assert evidence == MySQLSchemaEvidence(
        schema="source_schema",
        migration=87,
        tables={
            "assets": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=2, checksum=111),
            "workflow_versions": MySQLTableEvidence(row_count=3, checksum=222),
        },
        views=("current_labels",),
        trigger_count=0,
    )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_mysql_text_defaults.py =====
"""MySQL 拒绝 TEXT 列上的普通默认值，而我们的测试都跑在接受它的 SQLite 上。

2026-09-16：迁移 116 直接 ``model.__table__.create()`` 建两张新表，本地 55 条用例全绿，
发布到真 MySQL 上炸在
``ERROR 1101 (42000): BLOB, TEXT, GEOMETRY or JSON column 'ai_value_json' can't have a
default value``——那两张表有 11 个 ``Text`` + ``server_default`` 的列。

``mysql_metadata()` 本来就会把 TEXT 换成 LONGTEXT、把默认值包成 MySQL 8.0.13+ 的表达式
默认值 ``DEFAULT ('…')``；问题只是那条迁移绕开了它。这里把 DDL 编译出来检查，不需要
真的有 MySQL——真 MySQL 的用例在没有 MySQL 的机器上会整片跳过，指望不上。
"""
import pathlib
import re

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql
from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateTable

from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import mysql_metadata

#: 裸默认值：DEFAULT '…' 而不是 DEFAULT ('…')。前者 MySQL 建表就失败。
BARE_TEXT_DEFAULT = re.compile(r"(?:LONG|MEDIUM|TINY)?TEXT[^,\n]*\bDEFAULT\s+(?!\()", re.IGNORECASE)


def _ddl(table):
    return str(CreateTable(table).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))


def test_no_table_compiles_to_a_bare_text_default():
    """整库扫一遍：任何一张表出现裸 TEXT 默认值，发布都会死在迁移那一步。"""

    offenders = []
    for name, table in sorted(mysql_metadata().tables.items()):
        for line in _ddl(table).splitlines():
            if BARE_TEXT_DEFAULT.search(line):
                offenders.append(f"{name}: {line.strip()}")
    assert not offenders, "MySQL 不接受 TEXT 列的普通默认值：\n" + "\n".join(offenders)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", [
    "incremental_correction_cases",
    "incremental_correction_analyses",
])
def test_these_tables_are_legal_only_after_adaptation(name):
    """同一张表：直接拿模型编译出来是非法的，走 mysql_metadata 才合法。

    前半句就是当初炸掉发布的那份 DDL，留着它是为了说明适配不是可选项。
    """

    from app.database import Base

    raw = _ddl(Base.metadata.tables[name])
    assert BARE_TEXT_DEFAULT.search(raw), "模型直出的 DDL 本来就该是 MySQL 不接受的那种"

    adapted = _ddl(mysql_metadata().tables[name])
    assert "LONGTEXT" in adapted, "Text 列应当被换成 LONGTEXT"
    assert not BARE_TEXT_DEFAULT.search(adapted)
    # 默认值本身不能在适配中丢掉，否则插入路径的语义就变了。
    assert adapted.count("DEFAULT ('") >= 3


def test_no_upgrade_step_builds_tables_straight_from_a_model():
    """升级步里不许出现 ``__table__.create``——那正是绕开适配器的那个写法。

    这条是本文件里唯一会因为「迁移写错」而变红的用例：上面两条查的是适配器，
    而适配器一直是对的，错的是有人没用它。
    """

    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline

    source = pathlib.Path(mysql_baseline.__file__).read_text()
    body = source[source.index("def upgrade_mysql_schema"):]
    assert "__table__.create" not in body, (
        "升级步要用 mysql_metadata().tables[...].create()，"
        "直接用模型建表会把 TEXT 默认值原样发给 MySQL 而被 1101 拒绝"
    )
    assert "mysql_metadata()" in body


def test_the_models_themselves_still_carry_the_defaults_sqlite_relies_on():
    """适配是 MySQL 侧的事；模型上的 server_default 不该被为了迁移而删掉。"""

    from app.incremental_correction_models import IncrementalCorrectionCase

    column = IncrementalCorrectionCase.__table__.c.ai_value_json
    assert column.server_default is not None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_nas_assets_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Iterator

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool
from sqlalchemy import create_engine

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import Asset, AssetVersion, User


@contextmanager
def _context(tmp_path: Path) -> Iterator[dict[str, object]]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    source_dir = root / "采集任务交付文件" / "灵感图"
    source_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    image_path = source_dir / "room.png"
    Image.new("RGB", (3, 2), (120, 80, 40)).save(image_path)
    local_dir = tmp_path / "local"
    local_dir.mkdir()
    with sessions() as db:
        user = User(
            username="nas-owner",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="NAS 管理员",
            is_admin=True,
            role="admin",
        )
        db.add(user)
        db.commit()

    def override_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    import app.main as main_module

    original_settings = main_module.settings
    main_module.settings = SimpleNamespace(
        upload_dir=local_dir,
        nas_maps_root=root,
    )
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield {
            "client": TestClient(app),
            "sessions": sessions,
            "source_dir": source_dir,
            "image_path": image_path,
            "local_dir": local_dir,
            "main_module": main_module,
            "nas_root": root,
        }
    finally:
        main_module.settings = original_settings
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_import_nas_creates_reference_without_copying_and_serves_file(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with _context(tmp_path) as fixture:
        client = fixture["client"]
        response = client.post(
            "/api/assets/import-nas",
            json={
                "source_uri": r"\\nas\maps\采集任务交付文件\灵感图",
                "package_name": "NAS 灵感图",
                "category_key": "inspiration_image",
            },
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        item = response.json()["items"][0]
        assert item["storage_backend"] == "nas_maps"
        assert item["source_uri"] == "nas://maps/采集任务交付文件/灵感图/room.png"
        assert not list(fixture["local_dir"].iterdir())

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            asset = db.scalar(select(Asset).where(Asset.source_uri == item["source_uri"]))
            assert asset is not None
            version = db.scalar(select(AssetVersion).where(AssetVersion.asset_id == asset.id))
            assert version is not None
            assert version.storage_backend == "nas_maps"
            assert version.source_uri == asset.source_uri

        served = client.get(f"/api/assets/{item['id']}/file")
        assert served.status_code == 200
        assert served.headers["content-type"].startswith("image/png")
        assert served.content


def test_import_nas_is_idempotent_and_rejects_hash_drift(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with _context(tmp_path) as fixture:
        client = fixture["client"]
        payload = {
            "source_uri": "nas://maps/采集任务交付文件/灵感图/room.png",
            "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        }
        first = client.post("/api/assets/import-nas", json=payload)
        assert first.status_code == 200
        second = client.post("/api/assets/import-nas", json=payload)
        assert second.status_code == 200
        assert second.json()["items"][0]["duplicate"] is True

        fixture["image_path"].write_bytes(b"changed")
        drift = client.get(f"/api/assets/{first.json()['items'][0]['id']}/file")
        assert drift.status_code == 400
        assert "NAS_HASH_MISMATCH" in str(drift.json()["detail"])

def test_missing_nas_directory_names_the_broken_level_and_close_matches(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """路径写错时要指出断在哪一级、同级有哪些相近目录。

    2026-08-31：运营填的目录名少了「及数据提取」四个字（输入框还显示不全），
    系统只说「NAS 文件或目录不存在」，她无从判断是路径错了还是挂载/权限问题。
    """

    from app.main import _nas_missing_path_message

    parent = root / "采集任务交付文件" / "梁嘉欣"
    (parent / "【知识图谱标签识别】测试样本及数据提取").mkdir(parents=True)
    (parent / "【知识图谱标签识别】样本1-4x100").mkdir()

    message = _nas_missing_path_message(
        parent / "【知识图谱标签识别】测试样本", root
    )

    assert "路径不存在" in message
    assert "【知识图谱标签识别】测试样本" in message
    # 关键：把正确的那个名字直接摆出来
    assert "【知识图谱标签识别】测试样本及数据提取" in message

def test_import_nas_error_body_tells_operator_the_right_directory(
