def test_same_nas_source_can_serve_two_categories(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """同一张 NAS 图要能分别进入两个评测类目，各自独立评测。"""

    with _context(tmp_path) as fixture:
        client = fixture["client"]
        payload = {
            "source_uri": "nas://maps/采集任务交付文件/灵感图/room.png",
            "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        }
        first = client.post("/api/assets/import-nas", json=payload)
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        first_id = first.json()["items"][0]["id"]

        second = client.post(
            "/api/assets/import-nas",
            json={**payload, "category_key": "knowledge_graph"},
        )
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        body = second.json()
        assert not body.get("failed_files"), body.get("failed_files")
        second_id = body["items"][0]["id"]

        # 两个类目各自一条素材，互不覆盖
        assert second_id != first_id
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            assets = db.scalars(
                select(Asset).where(Asset.source_uri == payload["source_uri"])
            ).all()
            assert {asset.category_key for asset in assets} == {
                "inspiration_image",
                "knowledge_graph",
            }


def test_same_nas_source_stays_unique_inside_one_category(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """同一类目内重复引用仍然复用同一条素材，不会造出第二条。"""

    with _context(tmp_path) as fixture:
        client = fixture["client"]
        payload = {
            "source_uri": "nas://maps/采集任务交付文件/灵感图/room.png",
            "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        }
        first = client.post("/api/assets/import-nas", json=payload)
        second = client.post("/api/assets/import-nas", json=payload)
        assert first.status_code == 200 and second.status_code == 200
        assert second.json()["items"][0]["duplicate"] is True

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            assets = db.scalars(
                select(Asset).where(
                    Asset.source_uri == payload["source_uri"],
                    Asset.category_key == "inspiration_image",
                )
            ).all()
            assert len(assets) == 1


def test_concurrent_duplicate_import_returns_409_instead_of_500(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """2026-09-29：页面刷新后重复提交，和还在跑的上一次撞同一批文件，原来是 500。"""
    from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
    with _context(tmp_path) as fixture:
        client = fixture["client"]
        main_module = fixture["main_module"]

        def collide(*args, **kwargs):
            raise IntegrityError("INSERT INTO assets", {}, Exception("Duplicate entry for key 'assets.stored_name'"))

        monkeypatch.setattr(main_module, "_import_nas_candidates", collide)
        response = client.post(
            "/api/assets/import-nas",
            json={"source_uri": "nas://maps/采集任务交付文件/灵感图", "category_key": "inspiration_image"},
        )
        assert response.status_code == 409
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "NAS_IMPORT_CONFLICT"


def test_import_lock_name_is_stable_per_category_and_folder() -> None:
    from app.main import _nas_import_lock_name
    a = _nas_import_lock_name("knowledge_graph", "nas://maps/a/b")
    assert a == _nas_import_lock_name("knowledge_graph", "nas://maps/a/b")
    assert a != _nas_import_lock_name("inspiration_image", "nas://maps/a/b")
    assert len(a) <= 64

===== FILE backend/tests/test_nas_history_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import Asset, AssetVersion
from app.nas_history_migration import (
    NasHistoryMigrationError,
    apply_relink_plan,
    build_relink_plan,
    cleanup_verified_local_files,
    relink_plan_from_payload,
    relink_plan_payload,
    relink_verification_payload,
    verify_relink_plan,
)
import pytest


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _asset(local_dir: Path, *, name: str, content: bytes) -> Asset:
    stored_name = f"stored-{hashlib.sha256(name.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}.jpg"
    path = local_dir / stored_name
    path.write_bytes(content)
    return Asset(
        original_name=name,
        stored_name=stored_name,
        storage_backend="local",
        source_uri=None,
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=len(content),
        sha256=_sha256(path),
        category_key="inspiration_image",
    )


def test_build_relink_plan_matches_unique_hash_and_keeps_ambiguous_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    local_dir = tmp_path / "local"
    nas_root = tmp_path / "maps"
    source_root = nas_root / "历史素材"
    local_dir.mkdir()
    source_root.mkdir(parents=True)

    unique = _asset(local_dir, name="unique.jpg", content=b"unique")
    ambiguous = _asset(local_dir, name="renamed.jpg", content=b"ambiguous")
    (source_root / "unique.jpg").write_bytes(b"unique")
    (source_root / "copy-a.jpg").write_bytes(b"ambiguous")
    (source_root / "copy-b.jpg").write_bytes(b"ambiguous")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add_all([unique, ambiguous])
        db.commit()
        plan = build_relink_plan(
            db,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
            scan_roots=["历史素材"],
            generated_at=datetime(2026, 8, 19, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        )

    assert len(plan.matches) == 1
    assert plan.matches[0].asset_id == unique.id
    assert plan.matches[0].source_uri == "nas://maps/历史素材/unique.jpg"
    assert plan.summary["nas_ambiguous"] == 1
    assert plan.plan_hash


def test_apply_and_verify_relink_plan_records_nas_source_version(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    local_dir = tmp_path / "local"
    nas_root = tmp_path / "maps"
    source_root = nas_root / "历史素材"
    local_dir.mkdir()
    source_root.mkdir(parents=True)
    asset = _asset(local_dir, name="folder/item.jpg", content=b"same-image")
    (source_root / "folder").mkdir()
    (source_root / "folder" / "item.jpg").write_bytes(b"same-image")

    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(asset)
        db.commit()
        plan = build_relink_plan(
            db,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
            scan_roots=["历史素材"],
            generated_at=datetime(2026, 8, 19, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        )
        result = apply_relink_plan(
            db,
            plan=plan,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
            actor="migration-test",
        )
        db.commit()

        db.refresh(asset)
        version = db.scalar(
            select(AssetVersion).where(AssetVersion.asset_id == asset.id)
        )
        verification = verify_relink_plan(
            db,
            plan=plan,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
        )

    assert result == {"relinked": 1, "already_relinked": 0}
    assert asset.storage_backend == "nas_maps"
    assert asset.source_uri == "nas://maps/历史素材/folder/item.jpg"
    assert version is not None
    assert version.storage_backend == "nas_maps"
    assert version.source_uri == asset.source_uri
    assert verification.ok is True
    assert verification.cleanup_stored_names == (asset.stored_name,)
    verification_payload = relink_verification_payload(plan, verification)
    assert verification_payload["cleanup_total_bytes"] == len(b"same-image")
    assert verification_payload["cleanup_manifest_sha256"]


def test_cleanup_deletes_only_files_that_still_pass_all_checks(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    local_dir = tmp_path / "local"
    nas_root = tmp_path / "maps"
    source_root = nas_root / "历史素材"
    local_dir.mkdir()
    source_root.mkdir(parents=True)
    safe = _asset(local_dir, name="safe.jpg", content=b"safe")
    changed = _asset(local_dir, name="changed.jpg", content=b"changed")
    (source_root / "safe.jpg").write_bytes(b"safe")
    (source_root / "changed.jpg").write_bytes(b"changed")

    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add_all([safe, changed])
        db.commit()
        plan = build_relink_plan(
            db,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
            scan_roots=["历史素材"],
            generated_at=datetime(2026, 8, 19, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        )
        apply_relink_plan(
            db,
            plan=plan,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
            actor="migration-test",
        )
        db.commit()
        (source_root / "changed.jpg").write_bytes(b"nas-drift")

        verification = verify_relink_plan(
            db,
            plan=plan,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
        )
        result = cleanup_verified_local_files(
            db,
            plan=plan,
            verification=verification,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
            confirmed_plan_hash=plan.plan_hash,
        )

    assert verification.ok is False
    assert verification.cleanup_stored_names == (safe.stored_name,)
    assert result == {"deleted": 1, "preserved": 1}
    assert not (local_dir / safe.stored_name).exists()
    assert (local_dir / changed.stored_name).exists()


def test_relink_plan_payload_rejects_tampering(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    local_dir = tmp_path / "local"
    nas_root = tmp_path / "maps"
    source_root = nas_root / "历史素材"
    local_dir.mkdir()
    source_root.mkdir(parents=True)
    asset = _asset(local_dir, name="safe.jpg", content=b"safe")
    (source_root / "safe.jpg").write_bytes(b"safe")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(asset)
        db.commit()
        plan = build_relink_plan(
            db,
            upload_dir=local_dir,
            nas_root=nas_root,
            scan_roots=["历史素材"],
        )

    payload = relink_plan_payload(plan)
    assert relink_plan_from_payload(payload) == plan
    payload["summary"]["matched"] = 999  # type: ignore[index]
    with pytest.raises(NasHistoryMigrationError, match="哈希"):
        relink_plan_from_payload(payload)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_nas_storage.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from PIL import Image

from app.nas_storage import (
    NasStorageError,
    inspect_nas_file,
    normalize_nas_uri,
    resolve_asset_path,
    resolve_nas_uri,
)


def test_normalize_nas_uri_accepts_supported_unc_and_uri_forms() -> None:
    assert normalize_nas_uri(r"\\nas\maps\小聪\灵感图\a.jpg") == "nas://maps/小聪/灵感图/a.jpg"
    assert normalize_nas_uri("//192.168.1.51/maps/小聪/灵感图/a.jpg") == "nas://maps/小聪/灵感图/a.jpg"
    assert normalize_nas_uri("nas://maps/小聪/灵感图/a.jpg") == "nas://maps/小聪/灵感图/a.jpg"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "value",
    [
        r"\\other-host\maps\a.jpg",
        r"\\nas\other-share\a.jpg",
        "nas://other-share/a.jpg",
        "nas://maps/../secret.jpg",
        "nas://maps/%2e%2e/secret.jpg",
        "/mnt/label-nas/maps/a.jpg",
    ],
)
def test_normalize_nas_uri_rejects_unsafe_locations(value: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(NasStorageError):
        normalize_nas_uri(value)


def test_resolve_nas_uri_stays_inside_root_and_rejects_symlink_escape(tmp_path) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    nested = root / "样本"
    nested.mkdir(parents=True)
    image = nested / "a.jpg"
    image.write_bytes(b"image")

    assert resolve_nas_uri("nas://maps/样本/a.jpg", root) == image.resolve()

    outside = tmp_path / "outside.jpg"
    outside.write_bytes(b"outside")
    link = nested / "escape.jpg"
    link.symlink_to(outside)
    with pytest.raises(NasStorageError, match="符号链接"):
        resolve_nas_uri("nas://maps/样本/escape.jpg", root)


def test_inspect_nas_file_returns_metadata_and_sha256(tmp_path) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    root.mkdir()
    image = root / "sample.png"
    Image.new("RGB", (12, 8), (1, 2, 3)).save(image, format="PNG")

    info = inspect_nas_file("nas://maps/sample.png", root)

    assert info.uri == "nas://maps/sample.png"
    assert info.mime_type == "image/png"
    assert (info.width, info.height) == (12, 8)
    assert info.sha256 == hashlib.sha256(image.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def test_resolve_asset_path_uses_nas_source_when_present(tmp_path) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    root.mkdir()
    image = root / "sample.jpg"
    image.write_bytes(b"image")
    settings = SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path / "local", nas_maps_root=root)
    asset = SimpleNamespace(
        storage_backend="nas_maps",
        source_uri="nas://maps/sample.jpg",
        stored_name="nas-placeholder.jpg",
    )

    assert resolve_asset_path(asset, settings) == image.resolve()


def test_resolve_asset_path_uses_hash_named_history_fallback_when_nas_file_is_missing(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    root.mkdir()
    fallback_root = tmp_path / "history-fallback"
    fallback_root.mkdir()
    payload = b"immutable historical image"
    expected_sha = hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
    fallback = fallback_root / expected_sha
    fallback.write_bytes(payload)
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        upload_dir=tmp_path / "local",
        nas_maps_root=root,
        nas_history_fallback_root=fallback_root,
    )
    asset = SimpleNamespace(
        storage_backend="nas_maps",
        source_uri="nas://maps/removed/sample.jpg",
        stored_name="nas-placeholder.jpg",
        sha256=expected_sha,
    )

    assert resolve_asset_path(asset, settings) == fallback.resolve()


def test_resolve_asset_path_rejects_history_fallback_with_wrong_content(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    root.mkdir()
    fallback_root = tmp_path / "history-fallback"
    fallback_root.mkdir()
    expected_sha = hashlib.sha256(b"expected").hexdigest()
    (fallback_root / expected_sha).write_bytes(b"tampered")
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        upload_dir=tmp_path / "local",
        nas_maps_root=root,
        nas_history_fallback_root=fallback_root,
    )
    asset = SimpleNamespace(
        storage_backend="nas_maps",
        source_uri="nas://maps/removed/sample.jpg",
        stored_name="nas-placeholder.jpg",
        sha256=expected_sha,
    )

    with pytest.raises(NasStorageError) as error:
        resolve_asset_path(asset, settings)

    assert error.value.code == "NAS_HISTORY_FALLBACK_HASH_MISMATCH"


def test_resolve_asset_path_does_not_hide_changed_nas_file_with_fallback(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    root.mkdir()
    primary = root / "sample.jpg"
    primary.write_bytes(b"changed on NAS")
    fallback_root = tmp_path / "history-fallback"
    fallback_root.mkdir()
    expected_payload = b"frozen historical image"
    expected_sha = hashlib.sha256(expected_payload).hexdigest()
    (fallback_root / expected_sha).write_bytes(expected_payload)
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        upload_dir=tmp_path / "local",
        nas_maps_root=root,
        nas_history_fallback_root=fallback_root,
    )
    asset = SimpleNamespace(
        storage_backend="nas_maps",
        source_uri="nas://maps/sample.jpg",
        stored_name="nas-placeholder.jpg",
        sha256=expected_sha,
    )

    with pytest.raises(NasStorageError) as error:
        resolve_asset_path(asset, settings)

    assert error.value.code == "NAS_HASH_MISMATCH"


def test_sha256_file_cached_reads_once_and_reacts_to_content_change(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """同一个 NAS 文件在一次任务里会被反复校验，摘要只应真正算一次。"""
    from app import nas_storage

    image = tmp_path / "cached.jpg"
    image.write_bytes(b"first")
    nas_storage._digest_cache.clear()

    calls: list[str] = []
    real = nas_storage._sha256_file

    def counting(path):
        calls.append(str(path))
        return real(path)

    monkeypatch.setattr(nas_storage, "_sha256_file", counting)

    first = nas_storage.sha256_file_cached(image)
    second = nas_storage.sha256_file_cached(image)
    assert first == second == hashlib.sha256(b"first").hexdigest()
    assert len(calls) == 1, "第二次调用应命中缓存，不再跨 SMB 全量重读"

    # 文件被替换后 mtime_ns/大小都会变，必须重算而不是返回过期摘要。
    image.write_bytes(b"second-and-longer")
    changed = nas_storage.sha256_file_cached(image)
    assert changed == hashlib.sha256(b"second-and-longer").hexdigest()
    assert len(calls) == 2


def test_resolve_asset_path_verifies_digest_without_rereading(tmp_path) -> None:
    """resolve_asset_path 反复调用（锚图按锚点逐个调）时不应重复算摘要。"""
    from app import nas_storage

    root = tmp_path / "maps"
    root.mkdir()
    image = root / "anchor.jpg"
    image.write_bytes(b"anchor-bytes")
    nas_storage._digest_cache.clear()

    calls: list[str] = []
    real = nas_storage._sha256_file

    def counting(path):
        calls.append(str(path))
        return real(path)

    original = nas_storage._sha256_file
    nas_storage._sha256_file = counting
    try:
        settings = SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path / "local", nas_maps_root=root)
        asset = SimpleNamespace(
            storage_backend="nas_maps",
            source_uri="nas://maps/anchor.jpg",
            stored_name="nas-placeholder.jpg",
            sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"anchor-bytes").hexdigest(),
        )
        for _ in range(4):
            assert resolve_asset_path(asset, settings) == image.resolve()
    finally:
        nas_storage._sha256_file = original

    assert len(calls) == 1, f"4 次解析只应读一遍文件，实际读了 {len(calls)} 遍"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_acceptance_cohort.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import stat
from dataclasses import replace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.native_acceptance_cohort import (
    AcceptanceCohortError,
    CohortCandidate,
    load_cohort_candidates,
    private_cohort_manifest,
    select_3d_su_cohort,
    select_inspiration_cohort,
    validate_cohort_assets,
    write_private_cohort_manifest,
)
from app.native_source_run_discovery import (
    SourceRunEvidence,
    discover_latest_usable_run,
)
from test_native_source_run_discovery import _seed_source_history


def _source(*, item_ids: tuple[int, ...], failed: tuple[int, ...] = ()) -> SourceRunEvidence:
    from datetime import datetime, timezone

    return SourceRunEvidence(
        run_id=141,
        baseline_set_id=12,
        category_key="inspiration_image",
        sequence_no=5,
        status="partial_failed",
        total=len(item_ids),
        completed=len(item_ids) - len(failed),
        valid_predictions=len(item_ids) - len(failed),
        failed=len(failed),
        workflow_version_id=102,
        workflow_version="native-r141",
        workflow_canonical_hash="a" * 64,
        created_at=datetime(2026, 8, 29, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        item_ids=item_ids,
        failed_item_ids=failed,
        components=(),
        invocation_counts={"succeeded": 100},
    )


def _candidate(
    item_id: int,
    *,
    stratum: str = "L1",
    backend: str = "nas_maps",
    baseline_set_id: int = 12,
) -> CohortCandidate:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"asset-{item_id}".encode()).hexdigest()
    return CohortCandidate(
        item_id=item_id,
        asset_id=10_000 + item_id,
        asset_sha256=digest,
        storage_backend=backend,
        source_uri=(f"nas://maps/acceptance/{item_id}.jpg" if backend == "nas_maps" else None),
        stored_name=(f"{item_id}.jpg" if backend == "local" else None),
        truth_revision=0,
        truth_hash=hashlib.sha256(f"truth-{item_id}".encode()).hexdigest(),
        truth_contract_key="level.v1",
        stratum=stratum,
        baseline_set_id=baseline_set_id,
    )


def test_inspiration_selection_includes_failures_then_uses_sha_order() -> None:
    """Catches hiding source failures or selecting by unstable database order."""

    pool = [_candidate(item_id) for item_id in range(1, 61)]
    source = _source(
        item_ids=tuple(candidate.item_id for candidate in pool),
        failed=(57, 11),
    )

    cohort = select_inspiration_cohort(source, pool=pool, size=50)

    assert len(cohort.items) == 50
    assert len({item.item_id for item in cohort.items}) == 50
    failed_items = sorted(
        (candidate for candidate in pool if candidate.item_id in {57, 11}),
        key=lambda candidate: (candidate.asset_sha256, candidate.item_id),
    )
    assert [item.item_id for item in cohort.items[:2]] == [
        item.item_id for item in failed_items
    ]
    expected_remaining = sorted(
        (candidate for candidate in pool if candidate.item_id not in {57, 11}),
        key=lambda candidate: (candidate.asset_sha256, candidate.item_id),
    )[:48]
    assert [item.item_id for item in cohort.items[2:]] == [
        item.item_id for item in expected_remaining
    ]
    assert cohort.items[0].inclusion_reason == "source_failed_first"
    assert cohort.items[2].inclusion_reason == "sha256_order"


def test_3d_su_selection_is_stratified_and_input_order_independent() -> None:
    """Catches a 50-item sample silently dropping a quality-level stratum."""

    pool = [
        _candidate(item_id, stratum=level, baseline_set_id=11)
        for item_id, level in enumerate(
            ["L1"] * 28 + ["L2"] * 29 + ["L3"] * 46 + ["L4"] * 22,
            start=1,
        )
    ]
    source = replace(
        _source(item_ids=(1,)),
        run_id=132,
        baseline_set_id=11,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        workflow_version_id=87,
        workflow_version="native-r33",
        workflow_canonical_hash="b" * 64,
    )

    forward = select_3d_su_cohort(source, pool=pool, size=50)
    reversed_input = select_3d_su_cohort(
        source, pool=list(reversed(pool)), size=50
    )

    assert len(forward.items) == 50
    assert {item.stratum for item in forward.items} == {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"}
    assert forward.cohort_hash == reversed_input.cohort_hash
    assert [item.item_id for item in forward.items] == [
        item.item_id for item in reversed_input.items
    ]
    assert all(item.inclusion_reason == "proportional_stratum" for item in forward.items)


def test_manifest_separates_source_run_set_from_execution_cohort_set() -> None:
    """3D&SU 可从更大的真值池抽样，但不能把来源 run 的 set 当执行 set。"""

    source = replace(
        _source(item_ids=(1,)),
        run_id=132,
        baseline_set_id=9,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
        workflow_version_id=87,
        workflow_version="native-r33",
        workflow_canonical_hash="b" * 64,
    )
    pool = [
        _candidate(item_id, baseline_set_id=11)
        for item_id in range(1, 51)
    ]

    manifest = private_cohort_manifest(
        select_3d_su_cohort(source, pool=pool, size=50)
    )

    assert manifest["schema_version"] == "native-acceptance-cohort-v2"
    assert manifest["source_baseline_set_id"] == 9
    assert manifest["execution_baseline_set_id"] == 11


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("mutate", "code"),
    [
        (
            lambda pool: pool.__setitem__(
                1, replace(pool[1], asset_sha256=pool[0].asset_sha256)
            ),
            "cohort_asset_hash_duplicate",
        ),
        (
            lambda pool: pool.__setitem__(1, replace(pool[1], truth_hash="")),
            "cohort_truth_missing",
        ),
        (
            lambda pool: pool.__setitem__(
                1, replace(pool[1], source_uri="/tmp/private.jpg")
            ),
            "cohort_asset_locator_invalid",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_cohort_rejects_ambiguous_identity_or_unsafe_locator(mutate, code: str) -> None:
    """Catches duplicate billing identity, missing truth, and local-path escape."""

    pool = [_candidate(item_id) for item_id in range(1, 51)]
    mutate(pool)

    with pytest.raises(AcceptanceCohortError) as exc_info:
        select_inspiration_cohort(
            _source(item_ids=tuple(range(1, 51))),
            pool=pool,
            size=50,
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == code


def test_asset_validation_hashes_every_selected_file_before_calls(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Catches beginning paid execution with missing or corrupt cohort material."""

    nas_root = tmp_path / "nas"
    upload_root = tmp_path / "uploads"
    (nas_root / "acceptance").mkdir(parents=True)
    upload_root.mkdir()
    nas_bytes = b"nas-asset"
    local_bytes = b"local-asset"
    (nas_root / "acceptance" / "1.jpg").write_bytes(nas_bytes)
    (upload_root / "2.jpg").write_bytes(local_bytes)
    candidates = [
        replace(
            _candidate(1),
            asset_sha256=hashlib.sha256(nas_bytes).hexdigest(),
        ),
        replace(
            _candidate(2, backend="local"),
            asset_sha256=hashlib.sha256(local_bytes).hexdigest(),
        ),
    ]
    cohort = select_inspiration_cohort(
        _source(item_ids=(1, 2)), pool=candidates, size=2
    )

    validation = validate_cohort_assets(
        cohort, nas_root=nas_root, upload_root=upload_root
    )

    assert validation == {"checked": 2, "bytes": len(nas_bytes) + len(local_bytes)}
    (upload_root / "2.jpg").unlink()
    with pytest.raises(AcceptanceCohortError) as exc_info:
        validate_cohort_assets(
            cohort, nas_root=nas_root, upload_root=upload_root
        )
    assert exc_info.value.code == "cohort_asset_missing"


def test_private_manifest_has_stable_hash_and_no_absolute_path() -> None:
    pool = [_candidate(item_id) for item_id in range(1, 3)]
    cohort = select_inspiration_cohort(
        _source(item_ids=(1, 2)), pool=pool, size=2
    )

    manifest = private_cohort_manifest(cohort)

    assert manifest["cohort_hash"] == cohort.cohort_hash
    assert all(
        not str(item.get("source_uri", "")).startswith("/")
        for item in manifest["items"]
    )


def test_database_loader_uses_effective_truth_and_opaque_storage_identity() -> None:
    """Catches sampling stale truth or copying an absolute local asset path."""

    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_source_history(db)
            source = discover_latest_usable_run(
                db, category_key="inspiration_image"
            )

            candidates = load_cohort_candidates(db, source=source)

            assert [candidate.item_id for candidate in candidates] == list(
                source.item_ids
            )
            assert all(candidate.truth_revision == 0 for candidate in candidates)
            assert all(candidate.truth_hash for candidate in candidates)
            assert all(
                candidate.baseline_set_id == source.baseline_set_id
                for candidate in candidates
            )
            assert all(candidate.source_uri is None for candidate in candidates)
            assert all("/" not in (candidate.stored_name or "") for candidate in candidates)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_workflow_support_loader_includes_frozen_anchor_assets(tmp_path) -> None:
    """真实回归素材镜像必须包含机制冻结的锚图，而不只是待评 cohort。"""
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.models import Asset, WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion
    from app.native_acceptance_cohort import load_workflow_support_assets

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'support-assets.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            digest = hashlib.sha256(b"anchor").hexdigest()
            asset = Asset(
                original_name="anchor.jpg",
                stored_name="anchor.jpg",
                storage_backend="nas_maps",
                source_uri="nas://maps/anchors/anchor.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=6,
                sha256=digest,
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                status="uploaded",
            )
            definition = WorkflowDefinition(
                workflow_key="acceptance.support-assets",
                name="验收依赖",
                description="",
                owner="system:test",
                allowed_categories_json='["inspiration_image"]',
                status="active",
                created_by="system:test",
            )
            db.add_all([asset, definition])
            db.flush()
            content = {
                "enabled": True,
                "anchors": [
                    {
                        "level": "L1",
                        "asset_id": asset.id,
                        "mime_type": "image/jpeg",
                        "sha256": digest,
                    }
                ],
            }
            workflow = WorkflowVersion(
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                version="v1",
                status="active",
                workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
                step_manifest_json=canonical_json(
                    [
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": "anchor_calibration",
                            "kind": "rule_module",
                            "config": {
                                "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                                "component_snapshot": {
                                    "kind": "rule_module",
                                    "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                                    "content_json": canonical_json(content),
                                },
                            },
                        }
                    ]
                ),
                edge_manifest_json="[]",
                input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
                output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
                resource_policy_json="{}",
                canonical_hash="a" * 64,
                validation_report_json="{}",
                created_by="system:test",
            )
            db.add(workflow)
            db.flush()

            support = load_workflow_support_assets(db, workflow.id)

            assert len(support) == 1
            assert support[0].asset_id == asset.id
            assert support[0].asset_sha256 == digest
            assert support[0].storage_backend == "nas_maps"
            assert support[0].source_uri == "nas://maps/anchors/anchor.jpg"
            assert support[0].role == "anchor"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_private_manifest_writer_is_exclusive_and_owner_only(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Catches leaking private asset locators or silently overwriting a cohort."""

    cohort = select_inspiration_cohort(
        _source(item_ids=(1, 2)),
        pool=[_candidate(1), _candidate(2)],
        size=2,
    )
    path = tmp_path / "inspiration.json"

    write_private_cohort_manifest(path, cohort)

    assert stat.S_IMODE(path.stat().st_mode) == 0o600
    assert json.loads(path.read_text())["cohort_hash"] == cohort.cohort_hash
    with pytest.raises(AcceptanceCohortError) as exc_info:
        write_private_cohort_manifest(path, cohort)
    assert exc_info.value.code == "cohort_manifest_exists"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_baseline_failure_detail.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.native_baseline_regression import _native_failure_detail


class _DB:
    def __init__(self, attempt):
        self._attempt = attempt

    def scalar(self, _statement):
        return self._attempt


def _run(error_message: str = "") -> SimpleNamespace:
    manifest = {
        "nodes": [
            {"node_instance_id": "prompt_overseas_single", "display_name": "海外单体打标"},
            {"node_instance_id": "project_overseas_single", "display_name": "生成海外知识图谱写入载荷"},
        ]
    }
    return SimpleNamespace(
        id=4622,
        status="failed",
        error_code="kg_output_value_invalid",
        error_message=error_message,
        snapshot_json=json.dumps({"workflow": {"manifest": manifest}}),
    )


def test_failure_detail_reads_like_prose_and_carries_rejected_output() -> None:
    """运营在平台上要看到：哪个节点、什么原因、模型实际输出、该怎么办。"""

    attempt = SimpleNamespace(
        step_key="prompt_overseas_single",
        last_error_code="kg_output_value_invalid",
        last_error_message="color 最多允许 1 个值",
        checkpoint_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "failure": {
                    "code": "kg_output_value_invalid",
                    "evidence": {
                        "rejected_model_output": {"res_id": "7167", "color": "white,black"}
                    },
                }
            }
        ),
    )
    detail = _native_failure_detail(
        _DB(attempt), _run(), error_code="kg_output_value_invalid"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )

    assert detail["stage_label"] == "海外单体打标"
    assert detail["summary"] == "海外单体打标：模型输出的字段值不符合合同规则（color 最多允许 1 个值）"
    assert detail["rejected_model_output"]["color"] == "white,black"
    assert "提示词" in (detail["hint"] or "")
    # 页面上不该出现裸错误码
    assert "kg_output_value_invalid" not in detail["summary"]


def test_failure_detail_degrades_without_attempt_or_evidence() -> None:
    """没有失败 attempt（例如整轮被取消）时也要给出可读说明，不能抛错。"""

    detail = _native_failure_detail(
        _DB(None), _run(), error_code="workflow_run_canceled"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    assert detail["summary"] == "工作流：本轮被人工取消"
    assert detail["rejected_model_output"] is None
    assert detail["hint"] is None


def test_failure_detail_explains_stale_model_snapshot_without_model_call() -> None:
    """run178：400 条全倒在 model_config_snapshot_drift，条目上只有裸码、无提示。"""

    attempt = SimpleNamespace(
        step_key="prompt_overseas_single",
        last_error_code="model_config_snapshot_drift",
        last_error_message="当前模型供应商、协议、端点或模型身份已变化，请重新生成评测机制版本",
        checkpoint_json="{}",
    )
    detail = _native_failure_detail(
        _DB(attempt), _run(), error_code="model_config_snapshot_drift"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    assert detail["summary"].startswith("海外单体打标：该版本冻结的模型已过期")
    assert "重新生成版本" in (detail["hint"] or "")
    assert "不产生费用" in (detail["hint"] or "")
    assert "model_config_snapshot_drift" not in detail["summary"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_baseline_incident.py =====
"""Operator-authorized checkpoint recovery must preserve facts and liabilities."""
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import (AuditEvent, BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun,
                        ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation)
from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
from app.workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run
from test_batch_failure_breaker import shared_case
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case
from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route
from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix


def _incident_fixture(sessions, cases):
    with sessions() as db:
        aggregate = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun))
        aggregate.execution_snapshot_json = canonical_json({
            'real_execution_budget':cases[0]['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['real_execution_budget']})
        succeeded = db.get(ProductionRun,db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,cases[0]['attempt_id']).run_id)
        succeeded.status = 'succeeded'
        db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,cases[0]['attempt_id']).status = 'succeeded'
        db.get(BaselineRegressionItem,succeeded.source_id).status = 'completed'
        failed = db.get(ProductionRun,db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,cases[1]['attempt_id']).run_id)
        step = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,cases[1]['attempt_id'])
        step.status, step.last_error_code = 'failed', 'model_failure_streak_exhausted'
        terminalize_runtime_run(db,failed,status='failed',error_code='model_failure_streak_exhausted',error_message='incident')
        for case in cases[2:]:
            reserve_model_invocation(db,attempt_id=case['attempt_id'],node=case['node'],run_snapshot=case['run_snapshot'],
                invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'batch:1',endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'],transport_mode='batch')
        db.commit()
        return dict(baseline_run_id=aggregate.id, expected_workflow_id=aggregate.workflow_version_id,
            expected_total=5, expected_cost_cap=cases[0]['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['real_execution_budget']['max_cost_micros'],
            incident_key='test-batch-breaker-repair', actor='test:authorized')


def test_quiesce_resume_preserves_facts_fees_and_is_idempotent(shared_case):
    from app.native_baseline_incident import quiesce_baseline_incident, resume_baseline_incident
    sessions,cases = shared_case
    contract = _incident_fixture(sessions,cases)
    with sessions() as db:
        snapshots = {r.id:r.snapshot_json for r in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun))}
        fees = {r.id:(r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros) for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))}
        report = quiesce_baseline_incident(db,**contract)
        assert report['retained_completed'] == 1
        assert len(report['recoverable_run_ids']) == 4
        assert len(report['interrupted_invocation_ids']) == 3
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert quiesce_baseline_incident(db,**contract) == report
        assert not db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(['running','leased']))).all()
        for row in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)):
            assert row.status == 'failed' and row.error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
            assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)['failure']['retryable'] is True
            assert (row.estimated_cost_micros,row.actual_cost_micros) == fees[row.id]
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        resumed = resume_baseline_incident(db,**contract,limit=2)
        assert resumed['resumed'] == 2 and resumed['remaining'] == 2
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        resumed = resume_baseline_incident(db,**contract,limit=2)
        assert resumed['resumed'] == 2 and resumed['remaining'] == 0
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        before_count = db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionStepAttempt.id)))
        assert resume_baseline_incident(db,**contract,limit=2)['resumed'] == 0
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionStepAttempt.id))) == before_count == 9
        assert {r.id:r.snapshot_json for r in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun))} == snapshots
        assert {r.id:(r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros) for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))} == fees
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id))) == 5  # quiesce + four per-run retries
        assert db.get(BaselineRegressionRun,contract['baseline_run_id']).status == 'running'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('wrong', ['expected_workflow_id','expected_total','expected_cost_cap'])
def test_quiesce_refuses_wrong_target_or_budget(shared_case,wrong):
    from app.native_baseline_incident import quiesce_baseline_incident
    sessions,cases = shared_case
    contract = _incident_fixture(sessions,cases)
    contract[wrong] += 1
    with sessions() as db, pytest.raises(ValueError,match='contract'):
        quiesce_baseline_incident(db,**contract)


def test_recovery_requires_committed_incident_and_dry_run_can_rollback(shared_case):
    from app.native_baseline_incident import quiesce_baseline_incident, resume_baseline_incident
    sessions,cases = shared_case
    contract = _incident_fixture(sessions,cases)
    with sessions() as db:
        quiesce_baseline_incident(db,**contract)
        db.rollback()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert all(r.status == 'reserved' for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)))
        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='quiesce'):
            resume_baseline_incident(db,**contract,limit=2)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('field', ['max_cost_micros','baseline_run_id','baseline_item_id'])
def test_quiesce_rejects_child_budget_or_scope_drift(shared_case,field):
    from app.native_baseline_incident import quiesce_baseline_incident
    sessions,cases = shared_case
    contract = _incident_fixture(sessions,cases)
    with sessions() as db:
        child = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id))
        snapshot = json.loads(child.snapshot_json)
        target = snapshot['runtime_context']
        if field == 'max_cost_micros': target = target['real_execution_budget']
        target[field] += 1
        child.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
        db.flush()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='child.*contract'):
            quiesce_baseline_incident(db,**contract)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_baseline_incident_mysql.py =====
"""Real MySQL races for incident resume; no provider requests are dispatched."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import asdict
from threading import Barrier, Event

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import event, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.batch_inference import pricing_models_for_route
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.migrations.mysql_invariants import install_mysql_invariants
from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing
from app.models import (
    AuditEvent,
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    WorkflowModelInvocation,
)
from app.native_baseline_incident import (
    quiesce_baseline_incident,
    resume_baseline_incident,
)
from app.strategy_bundle import build_model_config_snapshot
from app.workflow_model_budget import (
    real_execution_budget_for_selection,
    real_execution_node_limits,
)
from app.workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run
from test_batch_failure_breaker import shared_case
from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import MODEL, route
from test_native_baseline_incident import _incident_fixture
from test_workflow_model_executor import _case


@pytest.fixture
def mysql_incident(mysql_engine, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    # The normal mysql_engine fixture creates and drops a guarded disposable
    # schema. Install immutable-record triggers after constructing frozen data.
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
    sessions = sessionmaker(
        bind=mysql_engine, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False,
    )
    with sessions() as db:
        run, attempt, _, model, _ = _case(db, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        node = snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'][0]
        model.model_id = MODEL
        model.pricing_contract_json = canonical_json(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
        model_snapshot = node['config']['model_snapshot']
        model_snapshot['model_id'] = MODEL
        model_snapshot['runtime_config'] = build_model_config_snapshot(model)
        model_snapshot.pop('snapshot_hash')
        model_snapshot['snapshot_hash'] = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(model_snapshot).encode(),
        ).hexdigest()
        runtime = snapshot['runtime_context']
        runtime['inference_route'] = route()
        models = pricing_models_for_route({model.id: model}, route())
        manifest = snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
        runtime['real_execution_node_limits'] = real_execution_node_limits(
            manifest, model_configs=models,
        )
        runtime['real_execution_budget'] = asdict(real_execution_budget_for_selection(
            manifest, 1, model_configs=models,
        ))
        run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
        run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
        db.commit()
        kwargs = dict(
            worker_id='worker-1', attempt_id=attempt.id,
            lease_token=attempt.lease_token, run_snapshot=snapshot, node=node,
            input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json), complete_parent=False,
        )
    sessions, cases = shared_case.__wrapped__((sessions, kwargs), monkeypatch)
    contract = _incident_fixture(sessions, cases)
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        install_mysql_invariants(connection)
    with sessions() as db:
        report = quiesce_baseline_incident(db, **contract)
        db.commit()
    return sessions, contract, report


def _bounded_lock_wait(db):
    db.connection().exec_driver_sql('SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout = 5')


def _is_entity_select(state, entity, *, name=None):
    return state.is_select and any(
        description.get('entity') is entity
        and (name is None or description.get('name') == name)
        for description in state.statement.column_descriptions
    )


def test_mysql_incident_resume_does_not_deadlock_sibling_finalization(mysql_incident):
    """Taking an item before the aggregate deadlocks a finishing earlier chunk."""
    sessions, contract, report = mysql_incident
    with sessions() as db:
        first = resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract, limit=1)
        db.commit()
    worker_run_id = first['resumed_run_ids'][0]
    target_run_id = report['recoverable_run_ids'][1]
    parent_locked = Event()
    operator_needs_parent = Event()

    def finish_earlier_chunk():
        with sessions() as db:
            _bounded_lock_wait(db)
            run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(
                ProductionRun.id == worker_run_id,
            ).with_for_update())
            db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun).where(
                BaselineRegressionRun.id == contract['baseline_run_id'],
            ).with_for_update())
            parent_locked.set()
            assert operator_needs_parent.wait(10), 'resume did not reach aggregate contention'
            # This real terminalization refreshes the shared aggregate by locking
            # all its items. The operator must not already own target's item.
            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db, run, status='failed', error_code='fixture_finalization_failure',
                error_message='zero-network sibling finalization',
            )
            db.commit()

    def resume_next_chunk():
        assert parent_locked.wait(10), 'sibling did not lock aggregate'
        with sessions() as db:
            _bounded_lock_wait(db)

            @event.listens_for(db, 'do_orm_execute')
            def before_locking_parent(state):
                # Synchronization only: observe a locking ORM read, never assert
                # emitted SQL or replace the real maintenance operation.
                if (_is_entity_select(state, BaselineRegressionRun)
                        and state.statement._for_update_arg is not None):
                    operator_needs_parent.set()

            @event.listens_for(db, 'before_flush')
            def before_updating_parent(session, flush_context, instances):
                # The broken implementation reaches the aggregate only after
                # owning the target item and preparing its counter UPDATE.
                if any(isinstance(row, BaselineRegressionRun) for row in session.dirty):
                    operator_needs_parent.set()

            result = resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract, limit=1)
            db.commit()
            return result

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as workers:
        finishing = workers.submit(finish_earlier_chunk)
        resuming = workers.submit(resume_next_chunk)
        result = resuming.result(timeout=20)
        finishing.result(timeout=20)
    assert result['resumed_run_ids'] == [target_run_id]
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, worker_run_id).status == 'failed'
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, target_run_id).status == 'queued'
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionStepAttempt.id)).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == target_run_id,
        )) == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 3


def test_mysql_concurrent_incident_resumes_create_one_attempt_per_child(mysql_incident):
    """Two operators discovering the same recovery set must not duplicate work."""
    sessions, contract, report = mysql_incident
    discovered = Barrier(2)

    def resume_once():
        with sessions() as db:
            _bounded_lock_wait(db)

            @event.listens_for(db, 'do_orm_execute', retval=True)
            def synchronize_discovery(state):
                result = state.invoke_statement()
                if _is_entity_select(state, AuditEvent, name='event_key'):
                    # Both SELECTs finish while no resume audits exist, forcing
                    # the real child-lock/idempotency recheck to settle the race.
                    discovered.wait(timeout=10)
                return result

            result = resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract, limit=4)
            db.commit()
            return result

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as workers:
        futures = [workers.submit(resume_once) for _ in range(2)]
        results = [future.result(timeout=20) for future in futures]
    resumed_ids = [run_id for result in results for run_id in result['resumed_run_ids']]
    assert sorted(resumed_ids) == report['recoverable_run_ids']
    assert len(resumed_ids) == len(set(resumed_ids)) == 4
    with sessions() as db:
        counts = dict(db.execute(select(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id, func.count(ProductionStepAttempt.id),
        ).group_by(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id)).all())
        assert all(counts[run_id] == 2 for run_id in report['recoverable_run_ids'])
        assert sum(counts.values()) == 9
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(
            AuditEvent.action == 'operator_incident_resume',
        )) == 4
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(BaselineRegressionItem.id)).where(
            BaselineRegressionItem.status == 'queued',
        )) == 4
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 3
        assert resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract, limit=4)['resumed'] == 0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_baseline_metric_projection.py =====
"""Metric projection preserves full snapshots and all truth semantics."""
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import BaselineRegressionItem
from app.native_baseline_regression import (
    NativeBaselineRegressionError, _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run,
    create_native_baseline_run, refresh_native_baseline_run,
)
from test_native_baseline_regression import _baseline
from test_stock_mechanism_selection import _mechanism


@pytest.fixture
def projection_db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine, autoflush=False) as db:
            yield db
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def seed(db):
    baseline = _baseline(db)
    run = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=baseline,
        workflow_version=_mechanism(db), actor="test", idempotency_key="projection")
    db.flush()
    return run, run.items[0]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("manual", [True, False, 1, "true", None])
def test_projection_matches_full_metrics_without_losing_dirty_final_item(projection_db, manual):
    db = projection_db
    run, item = seed(db)
    item.status = "completed"
    item.expected_result_json = json.dumps({"level": "L2", "style": ["现代", "简约"], "unknown": None})
    item.result_snapshot_json = json.dumps({
        "normalized_result": {"level": "L3", "style": ["现代"],
            "short_circuit_decision": {"estimated_saved_cost_micros": 23}},
        "manual_required": manual,
        "workflow_node_outputs": {"large_evidence": "e" * 1_000_000},
    })
    expected = refresh_native_baseline_run(run, items=[item])
    snapshot = item.result_snapshot_json
    actual = _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(db, run)
    assert actual == expected
    assert db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionItem.result_snapshot_json).where(
        BaselineRegressionItem.id == item.id)) == snapshot
    assert run.completed == 1
    assert run.status == ("partial_failed" if manual is True else "completed")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("snapshot", ["{}", "", '{"normalized_result":null}', '{"normalized_result":7}'])
def test_empty_and_nonobject_prediction_keep_existing_metrics(projection_db, snapshot):
    db = projection_db
    run, item = seed(db)
    item.result_snapshot_json = snapshot
    assert _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(db, run) == refresh_native_baseline_run(run, items=[item])


@pytest.mark.parametrize("snapshot", ["{bad", "[]", "null", '"text"'])
def test_invalid_snapshot_still_blocks_aggregation(projection_db, snapshot):
    db = projection_db
    run, item = seed(db)
    item.result_snapshot_json = snapshot
    with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as before:
        refresh_native_baseline_run(run, items=[item])
    with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as after:
        _refresh_locked_native_baseline_run(db, run)
    assert before.value.code == after.value.code == "baseline_result_snapshot_invalid"
    assert str(before.value) == str(after.value)


def test_content_cache_refreshes_status_truth_and_changed_snapshot(projection_db, monkeypatch):
    import hashlib
    from app import native_baseline_regression as native
    db = projection_db
    if db.get_bind().dialect.name == "sqlite":
        db.connection().connection.create_function('sha2', 2, lambda value, bits: hashlib.sha256(value.encode()).hexdigest())
    cache = native._MetricSnapshotCache()
    monkeypatch.setattr(native, '_metric_snapshot_cache', cache)
    run, item = seed(db)
    def aggregate():
        db.flush()
        rows = db.execute(native._native_metric_projection(run.id)).all()
        return native.compute_truth_metrics(native._cached_metric_rows(db, rows))
    assert aggregate() == native.compute_truth_metrics([native._metric_row(item)])
    item.status = 'completed'
    item.expected_result_json = '{"level":"L3","name":"新真值"}'
    item.result_snapshot_json = '{"normalized_result":{"level":"L3","name":"新真值"},"evidence":"原始证据"}'
    assert aggregate() == native.compute_truth_metrics([native._metric_row(item)])
    # Warm cache must not read the complete evidence a second time.
    from sqlalchemy import event
    statements = []
    def capture(conn, cursor, statement, parameters, context, executemany):
        statements.append(statement)
    event.listen(db.get_bind(), 'before_cursor_execute', capture)
    try:
        item.expected_result_json = '{"level":"L2"}'
        item.status = 'failed'
        assert aggregate() == native.compute_truth_metrics([native._metric_row(item)])
    finally:
        event.remove(db.get_bind(), 'before_cursor_execute', capture)
    selects = [s for s in statements if s.lstrip().upper().startswith('SELECT')]
    assert len(selects) == 1 and 'sha2' in selects[0]
    # Replacing a snapshot, even on the same item, is a cache miss.
    item.result_snapshot_json = '{"manual_required":true,"normalized_result":{"level":"L1"}}'
    assert aggregate() == native.compute_truth_metrics([native._metric_row(item)])


def test_cache_has_byte_and_entry_bounds_and_returns_isolated_payloads():
    from app.native_baseline_regression import _MetricSnapshotCache, _compact_metric_snapshot
    cache = _MetricSnapshotCache(max_bytes=6, max_entries=2)
    cache.put('a', b'aa'); cache.put('b', b'bb')
    assert cache.get('a') == b'aa'
    cache.put('c', b'cc')
    assert cache.get('b') is None
    cache.put('a', b'aaaaa')
    assert cache.get('c') is None and cache._bytes == 5
    cache.put('too-large', b'x' * 7)
    assert cache.get('too-large') is None and cache._bytes == 5
    value = _compact_metric_snapshot('{"manual_required":1,"normalized_result":{"level":"L2"}}')
    first = json.loads(value); first['normalized_result']['level'] = 'L5'
    assert json.loads(value) == {'manual_required':False,'normalized_result':{'level':'L2'},'workflow_status':None}
    running = json.loads(_compact_metric_snapshot('{"workflow_runtime":{"status":"failed"},"normalized_result":null}'))
    assert running == {'manual_required':False,'normalized_result':None,'workflow_status':'failed'}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_baseline_regression.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
)

from test_stock_mechanism_selection import _mechanism


def _baseline(db: Session, *, field_truth: bool = False) -> BaselineSet:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="native-stock.jpg",
        stored_name="native-stock.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=20,
        sha256="f" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    db.add(asset)
    baseline = BaselineSet(
        category_key="space_image",
        name="原生存量回归测试集",
        description="",
        default_expected_level="L1",
        fingerprint="a" * 64,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        BaselineSetItem(
            baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            expected_level=None if field_truth else "L2",
            expected_result_json=(
                canonical_json({"style": "现代", "is_single": 0})
                if field_truth
                else canonical_json({"level": "L2"})
            ),
            truth_contract_key=("kg.fields.v1" if field_truth else "level.v1"),
            asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
            ),
        )
    )
    db.flush()
    return baseline


def _add_level_truth_item(
    db: Session,
    baseline: BaselineSet,
    *,
    suffix: str,
) -> BaselineSetItem:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name=f"native-stock-{suffix}.jpg",
        stored_name=f"native-stock-{suffix}.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=21,
        sha256=(suffix[-1] if suffix[-1].isdigit() else "e") * 64,
        category_key=baseline.category_key,
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    item = BaselineSetItem(
        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
        asset_id=asset.id,
        expected_level="L2",
        expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
        truth_contract_key="level.v1",
        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
        ),
    )
    db.add(item)
    db.flush()
    return item


def test_create_native_baseline_run_creates_only_workflow_runs(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-stock-create.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)

            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-run-1",
            )
            duplicate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-stock-run-1",
            )
            db.flush()

            assert duplicate.id == run.id
            assert run.execution_engine == "native_workflow"
            assert run.workflow_version_id == workflow.id
            assert run.strategy_bundle_id is None
            assert len(run.items) == 1
            assert run.items[0].expected_result_json == canonical_json(
                {"level": "L2"}
            )
            assert run.items[0].truth_contract_key == "level.v1"
            assert run.items[0].production_run_id is not None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id))) == 0

            production = db.get(ProductionRun, run.items[0].production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            frozen = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            assert frozen["workflow"]["workflow_version_id"] == workflow.id
            assert frozen["workflow"]["canonical_hash"] == workflow.canonical_hash
            assert frozen["runtime_context"]["baseline_run_id"] == run.id
            assert frozen["runtime_context"]["baseline_item_id"] == run.items[0].id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_failed_native_children_terminalize_the_parent(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-failed-parent.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="2")
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-failed-parent",
            )

            for item in run.items:
                production = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
                assert production is not None
                terminalize_runtime_run(
                    db,
                    production,
                    status="failed",
                    error_code="model_provider_unavailable",
                    error_message="provider detail must-not-reach-baseline",
                )
                terminalize_runtime_run(
                    db,
                    production,
                    status="failed",
                    error_code="model_provider_unavailable",
                    error_message="provider detail must-not-reach-baseline",
                )

            db.flush()
            assert [item.status for item in run.items] == ["failed", "failed"]
            assert run.status == "failed"
            assert run.failed == 2
            assert run.completed == 2
            assert run.valid_predictions == 0
            assert run.finished_at is not None
            assert all(
                "must-not-reach-baseline" not in item.result_snapshot_json
                for item in run.items
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_manual_runtime_retry_reopens_failed_native_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_runtime import (
        retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint,
        terminalize_runtime_run,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-manual-retry.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            baseline_run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-manual-retry",
            )
            item = baseline_run.items[0]
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            first_attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence)
            )
            assert first_attempt is not None
            first_attempt.status = "failed"
            first_attempt.last_error_code = "prompt_placeholder_unresolved"
            first_attempt.last_error_message = "frozen prompt was incomplete"
            terminalize_runtime_run(
                db,
                production,
                status="failed",
                error_code="prompt_placeholder_unresolved",
                error_message="frozen prompt was incomplete",
            )
            assert item.status == "failed"
            assert baseline_run.status == "failed"

            retry_failed_run_from_checkpoint(db, production.id)

            assert item.status == "queued"
            assert item.result_snapshot_json == "{}"
            assert item.error_message == ""
            assert item.finished_at is None
            assert baseline_run.status == "running"
            assert baseline_run.failed == 0
            assert baseline_run.finished_at is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_terminal_child_recovery_locks_run_before_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    """MySQL workers must not invert the normal run -> item lock order."""

    from datetime import datetime, timezone

    from sqlalchemy import event

    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        create_native_baseline_run,
        reconcile_terminal_native_baseline_children,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-lock-order.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    statements: list[str] = []

    def record_statement(
        _connection,
        _cursor,
        statement,
        _parameters,
        _context,
        _executemany,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(" ".join(statement.lower().split()))

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-lock-order",
            )
            production = db.get(
                ProductionRun,
                run.items[0].production_run_id,
            )
            assert production is not None
            production.status = "failed"
            production.error_code = "model_execution_unexpected"
            production.finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            db.flush()

            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)
            try:
                assert reconcile_terminal_native_baseline_children(db) == 1
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)

            candidate_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith(
                    "select production_runs.id from production_runs join"
                )
            )
            run_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select production_runs.id,")
                and "where production_runs.id =" in statement
            )
            item_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_items.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_items.id =" in statement
            )
            assert candidate_index < run_lock_index < item_lock_index
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_success_finalizer_locks_parent_before_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Parallel child completion must serialize through the parent run lock."""

    from sqlalchemy import event

    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        create_native_baseline_run,
        finalize_native_baseline_item,
    )
    from app.workflow_result_finalizer import NativeEvaluationFact

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-parent-lock-order.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    statements: list[str] = []

    def record_statement(
        _connection,
        _cursor,
        statement,
        _parameters,
        _context,
        _executemany,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(" ".join(statement.lower().split()))

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-parent-lock-order",
            )
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, run.items[0].production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            production.status = "succeeded"
            db.flush()

            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)
            try:
                finalize_native_baseline_item(
                    db,
                    production,
                    NativeEvaluationFact(
                        normalized_result={"level": "L2"},
                        result_hash="b" * 64,
                        workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                        run_id=production.id,
                        node_outputs={"final": {"status": "succeeded"}},
                        evaluation_job_id=201,
                        evaluation_result_id=202,
                    ),
                )
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)

            parent_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_runs.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_runs.id =" in statement
            )
            item_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_items.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_items.id =" in statement
            )
            assert parent_lock_index < item_lock_index
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_runtime_output_persistence_locks_parent_before_baseline_item(tmp_path) -> None:
    """The pre-finalizer snapshot write must follow the same parent-first order."""

    from sqlalchemy import event

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_runtime import persist_baseline_workflow_outputs

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-runtime-output-lock-order.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    statements: list[str] = []

    def record_statement(
        _connection,
        _cursor,
        statement,
        _parameters,
        _context,
        _executemany,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(" ".join(statement.lower().split()))

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-runtime-output-lock-order",
            )
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, run.items[0].production_run_id)
            assert production is not None
            production.status = "succeeded"
            db.flush()

            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)
            try:
                persist_baseline_workflow_outputs(db, production)
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record_statement)

            parent_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_runs.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_runs.id =" in statement
            )
            item_lock_index = next(
                index
                for index, statement in enumerate(statements)
                if statement.startswith("select baseline_regression_items.id,")
                and "where baseline_regression_items.id =" in statement
            )
            assert parent_lock_index < item_lock_index
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
