def test_native_analysis_uses_only_human_node_evidence_and_accepts_field_truth(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_regression import correction_input_snapshot
    from app.workflow_correction import analyze_workflow_corrections

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)

            snapshot = correction_input_snapshot(
                case["source_run"], [case["source_item"]]
            )
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )

            assert snapshot["schema_version"] == "baseline-correction-input-v3"
            assert snapshot["items"][0]["expected_result"] == {"style": "现代"}
            assert analysis.blockers == ()
            assert analysis.baseline_workflow_version_id == case[
                "base_workflow"
            ].id
            assert [
                (
                    group.node_instance_id,
                    group.asset_key,
                    group.component_version_id,
                    group.field_path,
                    group.evidence_count,
                )
                for group in analysis.groups
            ] == [
                (
                    "prompt_1",
                    "prompt_c",
                    case["base_prompt"].id,
                    "style",
                    1,
                ),
                (
                    "redline_1",
                    "eligibility_redline",
                    case["base_rule"].id,
                    "hit",
                    1,
                ),
            ]
            assert all(
                sample["corrector"] == "运营甲"
                for group in analysis.groups
                for sample in group.typical_samples
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_recommendation_reuses_proven_prompt_and_generates_rule_version(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        recommend_component_versions,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )

            recommendations = recommend_component_versions(db, analysis)

            by_node = {item.node_instance_id: item for item in recommendations}
            assert by_node["prompt_1"].mode == "reused"
            assert (
                by_node["prompt_1"].candidate_version_id
                == case["better_prompt"].id
            )
            assert by_node["redline_1"].mode == "generated"
            generated_rule = db.get(
                RuleModuleVersion,
                by_node["redline_1"].candidate_version_id,
            )
            assert generated_rule is not None
            assert generated_rule.asset_id == case["rule_asset"].id
            assert generated_rule.id != case["base_rule"].id
            assert len(by_node["prompt_1"].evidence_hash) == 64
            assert len(by_node["redline_1"].evidence_hash) == 64
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("stale_editor", [False, True])
def test_native_recommendation_generates_distinct_prompt_from_human_evidence_when_working_copy_is_unchanged(
    tmp_path, stale_editor,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        WorkflowCorrectionAnalysis,
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        recommend_component_versions,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            alternate_result = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationResult).where(
                    EvaluationResult.workflow_version_id
                    == case["alternate_workflow"].id
                )
            )
            assert alternate_result is not None
            alternate_outputs = json.loads(
                alternate_result.workflow_node_outputs_json
            )
            alternate_outputs["prompt_1"]["output"]["style"] = "古典"
            alternate_result.workflow_node_outputs_json = canonical_json(
                alternate_outputs
            )

            working = get_component_working_copy(db, case["prompt_asset"].id)
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=case["prompt_asset"].id,
                expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
                content={
                    "stage": case["base_prompt"].stage,
                    "system_prompt": case["base_prompt"].system_prompt,
                    "user_prompt": case["base_prompt"].user_prompt,
                    "rubric_version": case["base_prompt"].rubric_version,
                },
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            if stale_editor:
                # An old, untouched editor must not replace the frozen run's
                # prompt/anchor instructions as though it were an operator edit.
                old_prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, working.base_prompt_version_id)
                if old_prompt.id == case["base_prompt"].id:
                    old_prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, case["better_prompt"].id)
                working.base_prompt_version_id = old_prompt.id
                working.content_json = canonical_json({
                    "stage": old_prompt.stage, "system_prompt": old_prompt.system_prompt,
                    "user_prompt": old_prompt.user_prompt, "rubric_version": old_prompt.rubric_version,
                })
                db.flush()
            frozen_working = get_component_working_copy(
                db, case["prompt_asset"].id
            )
            frozen_working_state = (
                frozen_working.content_json,
                frozen_working.lock_version,
                frozen_working.base_prompt_version_id,
            )
            full_analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )
            prompt_analysis = WorkflowCorrectionAnalysis(
                groups=tuple(
                    group
                    for group in full_analysis.groups
                    if group.node_instance_id == "prompt_1"
                ),
                blockers=full_analysis.blockers,
                baseline_workflow_version_id=(
                    full_analysis.baseline_workflow_version_id
                ),
                baseline_run_id=full_analysis.baseline_run_id,
                category_key=full_analysis.category_key,
                selected_item_ids=full_analysis.selected_item_ids,
            )

            first = recommend_component_versions(db, prompt_analysis)
            replayed = recommend_component_versions(db, prompt_analysis)

            assert len(first) == 1
            assert first[0].mode == "generated"
            assert first[0].candidate_version_id != case["base_prompt"].id
            assert replayed[0].candidate_version_id == first[0].candidate_version_id
            candidate = db.get(PromptVersion, first[0].candidate_version_id)
            assert candidate is not None
            assert candidate.category_key == "space_image"
            assert candidate.stage == case["base_prompt"].stage
            assert candidate.source == "automatic_correction"
            assert "style" in candidate.system_prompt
            assert "人工确认是现代风格" in candidate.system_prompt
            binding = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id
                    == case["prompt_asset"].id,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == candidate.id,
                )
            )
            assert binding is not None
            unchanged_working = get_component_working_copy(
                db, case["prompt_asset"].id
            )
            assert (
                unchanged_working.content_json,
                unchanged_working.lock_version,
                unchanged_working.base_prompt_version_id,
            ) == frozen_working_state
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_recommendation_generates_distinct_rule_module_from_human_evidence_when_working_copy_is_unchanged(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        WorkflowCorrectionAnalysis,
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        recommend_component_versions,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            working = get_component_working_copy(db, case["rule_asset"].id)
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=case["rule_asset"].id,
                expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
                content=json.loads(case["base_rule"].content_json),
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            frozen_working = get_component_working_copy(
                db, case["rule_asset"].id
            )
            frozen_working_state = (
                frozen_working.content_json,
                frozen_working.lock_version,
                frozen_working.base_rule_module_version_id,
            )
            full_analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )
            rule_analysis = WorkflowCorrectionAnalysis(
                groups=tuple(
                    group
                    for group in full_analysis.groups
                    if group.node_instance_id == "redline_1"
                ),
                blockers=full_analysis.blockers,
                baseline_workflow_version_id=(
                    full_analysis.baseline_workflow_version_id
                ),
                baseline_run_id=full_analysis.baseline_run_id,
                category_key=full_analysis.category_key,
                selected_item_ids=full_analysis.selected_item_ids,
            )

            first = recommend_component_versions(db, rule_analysis)
            replayed = recommend_component_versions(db, rule_analysis)

            assert len(first) == 1
            assert first[0].mode == "generated"
            assert first[0].candidate_version_id != case["base_rule"].id
            assert replayed[0].candidate_version_id == first[0].candidate_version_id
            candidate = db.get(
                RuleModuleVersion, first[0].candidate_version_id
            )
            assert candidate is not None
            guidance = json.loads(candidate.content_json)[
                "correction_guidance"
            ]
            assert guidance["source_version_id"] == case["base_rule"].id
            assert guidance["fields"][0]["field_path"] == "hit"
            assert guidance["fields"][0]["operator_guidance"] == [
                "规则漏判"
            ]
            binding = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id
                    == case["rule_asset"].id,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.rule_module_version_id
                    == candidate.id,
                )
            )
            assert binding is not None
            unchanged_working = get_component_working_copy(
                db, case["rule_asset"].id
            )
            assert (
                unchanged_working.content_json,
                unchanged_working.lock_version,
                unchanged_working.base_rule_module_version_id,
            ) == frozen_working_state
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_candidate_workflow_changes_only_recommended_components_and_runs_once(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
        WorkflowCorrectionError,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )
            recommendations = recommend_component_versions(db, analysis)

            candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
                db,
                case["base_workflow"].id,
                recommendations,
                case["correction"].id,
                "ops",
            )
            replayed_candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
                db,
                case["base_workflow"].id,
                recommendations,
                case["correction"].id,
                "ops",
            )

            assert replayed_candidate.id == candidate.id
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowCorrectionError) as changed_retry:
                compose_candidate_workflow(
                    db,
                    case["base_workflow"].id,
                    recommendations[:1],
                    case["correction"].id,
                    "ops",
                )
            assert getattr(changed_retry.value, "code", None) == (
                "workflow_candidate_idempotency_conflict"
            )
            base_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(case["base_workflow"])
            candidate_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(candidate)
            assert candidate_manifest["edges"] == base_manifest["edges"]
            assert candidate_manifest.get("layout") == base_manifest.get("layout")
            base_prompt_config = _node(base_manifest, "prompt_1")["config"]
            candidate_prompt_config = _node(candidate_manifest, "prompt_1")[
                "config"
            ]
            assert candidate_prompt_config["model_registry_id"] == (
                base_prompt_config["model_registry_id"]
            )
            assert candidate_prompt_config["max_tokens"] == base_prompt_config[
                "max_tokens"
            ]
            assert candidate_prompt_config["thinking_level"] == (
                base_prompt_config["thinking_level"]
            )
            assert candidate_prompt_config["prompt_version_id"] == case[
                "better_prompt"
            ].id
            assert _node(candidate_manifest, "redline_1")["config"][
                "rule_module_version_id"
            ] != case["base_rule"].id
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(BaselineCorrectionComponentCandidate.id)).where(
                    BaselineCorrectionComponentCandidate.correction_run_id
                    == case["correction"].id
                )
            ) == 2

            candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db, case["correction"], "ops"
            )
            replayed_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db, case["correction"], "ops"
            )
            assert replayed_run.id == candidate_run.id
            assert candidate_run.execution_engine == "native_workflow"
            assert candidate_run.workflow_version_id == candidate.id
            assert candidate_run.previous_run_id == case["source_run"].id
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(BaselineRegressionRun.id)).where(
                    BaselineRegressionRun.previous_run_id
                    == case["source_run"].id
                )
            ) == 1

            candidate_run.status = "completed"
            candidate_run.completed = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.valid_predictions = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.metrics_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-truth-metrics-v1",
                    "total": 1,
                    "completed": 1,
                    "pending": 0,
                    "valid_predictions": 1,
                    "failed": 0,
                    "manual_required": 0,
                    "coverage": 1.0,
                    "field_metrics": {"style": {"accuracy": 1.0, "compared": 1, "correct": 1}},
                    "field_aggregate": {"accuracy": 1.0},
                    "level_metrics": None,
                }
            )
            refresh_correction_run(db, case["correction"])
            refresh_correction_run(db, case["correction"])

            assert case["correction"].status == "awaiting_decision"
            assert case["correction"].stage == "decision"
            assert case["correction"].regression_run_id == candidate_run.id
            binding = db.scalar(
                select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key
                    == "space_image"
                )
            )
            assert binding is not None
            assert binding.workflow_version_id == case["base_workflow"].id
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(BaselineRegressionRun.id)).where(
                    BaselineRegressionRun.previous_run_id
                    == case["source_run"].id
                )
            ) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_candidate_workflow_rejects_cross_category_version_without_copy(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        ComponentRecommendation,
        WorkflowCorrectionError,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="material_image",
                    display_name="材质图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                    preprocess_config_json="{}",
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="ops",
                )
            )
            foreign_asset = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="material_image",
                asset_key="prompt_c",
                kind="prompt",
                display_name="材质图提示词 C",
                prompt_stage="C",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=foreign_asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content={
                    "stage": "C",
                    "system_prompt": "材质图提示词。",
                    "user_prompt": "返回材质字段。",
                    "rubric_version": "rubric-v1",
                },
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            foreign_version = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=foreign_asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )
            recommendation = ComponentRecommendation(
                node_instance_id="prompt_1",
                asset_key="prompt_c",
                source_version_id=case["base_prompt"].id,
                candidate_version_id=foreign_version.id,
                mode="reused",
                evidence_hash="9" * 64,
            )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowCorrectionError) as exc_info:
                compose_candidate_workflow(
                    db,
                    case["base_workflow"].id,
                    [recommendation],
                    case["correction"].id,
                    "ops",
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "workflow_candidate_category_mismatch"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_candidate_without_comparable_accuracy_cannot_be_approved(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )
            candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
                db,
                case["base_workflow"].id,
                recommend_component_versions(db, analysis),
                case["correction"].id,
                "ops",
            )
            candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db, case["correction"], "ops"
            )
            assert candidate_run.workflow_version_id == candidate.id
            candidate_run.status = "completed"
            candidate_run.completed = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.valid_predictions = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.metrics_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-truth-metrics-v1",
                    "total": 1,
                    "completed": 1,
                    "pending": 0,
                    "valid_predictions": 1,
                    "failed": 0,
                    "manual_required": 0,
                    "coverage": 1.0,
                    "field_metrics": {},
                    "field_aggregate": {},
                    "level_metrics": None,
                }
            )

            refresh_correction_run(db, case["correction"])

            assert case["correction"].status == "awaiting_decision"
            report = json.loads(case["correction"].report_json)[
                "candidate_regression"
            ]
            assert report["approval_allowed"] is False
            assert report["recommendation"] == "reject"
            assert {
                item["code"] for item in report["regressions"]
            } == {"candidate_accuracy_unavailable"}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_level_candidate_uses_level_accuracy_before_empty_field_metrics(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )
            compose_candidate_workflow(
                db,
                case["base_workflow"].id,
                recommend_component_versions(db, analysis),
                case["correction"].id,
                "ops",
            )
            candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db, case["correction"], "ops"
            )
            case["source_run"].metrics_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-truth-metrics-v1",
                    "truth_kind": "level",
                    "field_aggregate": {
                        "accuracy": 0.0,
                        "compared": 0,
                    },
                    "level_metrics": {"exact_accuracy": 0.38},
                }
            )
            candidate_run.status = "completed"
            candidate_run.completed = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.valid_predictions = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.metrics_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-truth-metrics-v1",
                    "truth_kind": "level",
                    "field_aggregate": {
                        "accuracy": 0.0,
                        "compared": 0,
                    },
                    "level_metrics": {"exact_accuracy": 0.30},
                }
            )

            refresh_correction_run(db, case["correction"])

            report = json.loads(case["correction"].report_json)[
                "candidate_regression"
            ]
            assert report["primary_accuracy_delta"] == pytest.approx(-0.08)
            assert report["approval_allowed"] is False
            assert report["recommendation"] == "reject"
            assert {
                item["code"] for item in report["regressions"]
            } == {"candidate_accuracy_regressed"}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_background_orchestration_uses_node_pipeline_without_model_call(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.main import _run_baseline_correction_background

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            correction_id = case["correction"].id
            db.commit()

        _run_baseline_correction_background(correction_id, bind=engine)

        with Session(engine) as db:
            correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert correction is not None
            assert correction.status == "processing"
            assert correction.stage == "regression"
            assert correction.candidate_workflow_version_id is not None
            assert correction.regression_run_id is not None
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(BaselineCorrectionComponentCandidate.id)).where(
                    BaselineCorrectionComponentCandidate.correction_run_id
                    == correction.id
                )
            ) == 2
            candidate_run_id = correction.regression_run_id

        _run_baseline_correction_background(correction_id, bind=engine)

        with Session(engine) as db:
            correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert correction is not None
            assert correction.regression_run_id == candidate_run_id
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(BaselineRegressionRun.id)).where(
                    BaselineRegressionRun.previous_run_id
                    == correction.baseline_run_id
                )
            ) == 1

            candidate_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, candidate_run_id)
            assert candidate_run is not None
            candidate_run.status = "completed"
            candidate_run.completed = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.valid_predictions = candidate_run.total
            candidate_run.metrics_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-truth-metrics-v1",
                    "total": 1,
                    "completed": 1,
                    "pending": 0,
                    "valid_predictions": 1,
                    "failed": 0,
                    "manual_required": 0,
                    "coverage": 1.0,
                    "field_metrics": {"style": {"accuracy": 1.0, "compared": 1, "correct": 1}},
                    "field_aggregate": {"accuracy": 1.0},
                    "level_metrics": None,
                }
            )
            refresh_correction_run(db, correction)
            assert correction.status == "awaiting_decision"
            db.commit()

        _run_baseline_correction_background(correction_id, bind=engine)

        with Session(engine) as db:
            correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert correction is not None
            assert correction.status == "awaiting_decision"
            assert correction.stage == "decision"
            assert correction.regression_run_id == candidate_run_id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_correction_api_freezes_workflow_identity_and_starts_candidate(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            db.delete(case["correction"])
            source_run_id = case["source_run"].id
            source_item_id = case["source_item"].id
            source_workflow_hash = case["base_workflow"].canonical_hash
            db.commit()

        def override_db():
            with SessionForTest() as db:
                yield db

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: SimpleNamespace(
            username="ops", role="admin", active=True, is_admin=True
        )
        client = TestClient(app)

        response = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{source_run_id}/corrections",
            json={
                "item_ids": [source_item_id],
                "idempotency_key": "native-correction-api-1",
            },
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        payload = response.json()
        assert "candidate_workflow_version_id" in payload
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, payload["id"])
            assert correction is not None
            assert correction.candidate_workflow_version_id is not None
            assert correction.regression_run_id is not None
            frozen = json.loads(correction.input_snapshot_json)
            assert frozen["workflow_canonical_hash"] == source_workflow_hash
            assert "strategy_canonical_id" not in frozen
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_correction_api_requires_regression_run_permission(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    actor = {
        "user": User(
            username="viewer",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="只读成员",
            role="viewer",
            is_admin=False,
        )
    }
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            db.delete(case["correction"])
            source_run_id = case["source_run"].id
            source_item_id = case["source_item"].id
            db.commit()

        def override_db():
            with SessionForTest() as db:
                yield db

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: actor["user"]
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            "app.main._run_baseline_correction_background",
            lambda *_args, **_kwargs: None,
        )
        client = TestClient(app)
        request = {
            "item_ids": [source_item_id],
            "idempotency_key": "native-correction-rbac-1",
        }

        denied = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{source_run_id}/corrections",
            json=request,
        )

        assert denied.status_code == 403, denied.text
        assert denied.json()["detail"] == "缺少权限：regressions:run"
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(BaselineCorrectionRun.id))) == 0

        actor["user"] = User(
            username="manager",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="项目管理员",
            role="product_rd",
            is_admin=False,
        )
        allowed = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{source_run_id}/corrections",
            json=request,
        )

        assert allowed.status_code == 200, allowed.text
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, allowed.json()["id"])
            assert correction is not None
            assert correction.created_by == "manager"
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_correction_retry_requires_regression_run_permission(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    actor = {
        "user": User(
            username="viewer",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="只读成员",
            role="viewer",
            is_admin=False,
        )
    }
    background_calls: list[int] = []
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            correction = case["correction"]
            correction.status = "failed"
            correction.stage = "analysis"
            correction.progress = 10
            correction.error_code = "provider_failed"
            correction.error_message = "provider failed"
            correction.attempt_count = 1
            correction_id = correction.id
            db.commit()
            audit_count_before = db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)))

        def override_db():
            with SessionForTest() as db:
                yield db

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: actor["user"]
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            "app.main._run_baseline_correction_background",
            lambda row_id, *_args, **_kwargs: background_calls.append(row_id),
        )
        client = TestClient(app)

        denied = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}/retry"
        )

        assert denied.status_code == 403, denied.text
        assert denied.json()["detail"] == "缺少权限：regressions:run"
        assert background_calls == []
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            frozen = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert frozen is not None
            assert frozen.status == "failed"
            assert frozen.attempt_count == 1
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id))) == audit_count_before

        actor["user"] = User(
            username="manager",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="项目管理员",
            role="product_rd",
            is_admin=False,
        )
        allowed = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}/retry"
        )

        assert allowed.status_code == 200, allowed.text
        assert allowed.json()["attempt_count"] == 2
        assert background_calls == [correction_id]
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            retried = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert retried is not None
            assert retried.status == "processing"
            assert retried.attempt_count == 2
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id))) == (
                audit_count_before + 1
            )
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_baseline_detail_exposes_structured_truth_and_field_deviation(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            case["source_item"].result_snapshot_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "native-baseline-item-result-v1",
                    "normalized_result": {"style": "古典"},
                }
            )
            source_run_id = case["source_run"].id
            db.commit()

        def override_db():
            with SessionForTest() as db:
                yield db

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: SimpleNamespace(
            username="ops", role="admin", active=True
        )
        payload = TestClient(app).get(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{source_run_id}"
        )

        assert payload.status_code == 200, payload.text
        item = payload.json()["items"][0]
        assert item["truth_kind"] == "structured_fields"
        assert item["expected_result"] == {"style": "现代"}
        assert item["normalized_result"] == {"style": "古典"}
        assert item["truth_contract_key"] == "field.object.v1"
        assert item["field_deviations"] == ["style"]
        assert item["deviation"] is True

        deviations = TestClient(app).get(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{source_run_id}?deviations_only=true"
        )
        assert deviations.status_code == 200, deviations.text
        assert [row["id"] for row in deviations.json()["items"]] == [
            item["id"]
        ]
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_candidate_human_approval_never_switches_incremental_pointer(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import admin_user, app, current_user
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db,
                case["source_run"].id,
                [case["source_item"].id],
            )
            recommendations = recommend_component_versions(db, analysis)
            candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
                db,
                case["base_workflow"].id,
                recommendations,
                case["correction"].id,
                "ops",
            )
            candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db, case["correction"], "ops"
            )
            candidate_run.status = "completed"
            candidate_run.completed = 1
            candidate_run.valid_predictions = 1
            candidate_run.metrics_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-truth-metrics-v1",
                    "total": 1,
                    "completed": 1,
                    "pending": 0,
                    "valid_predictions": 1,
                    "failed": 0,
                    "manual_required": 0,
                    "coverage": 1.0,
                    "field_metrics": {"style": {"accuracy": 1.0, "compared": 1, "correct": 1}},
                    "field_aggregate": {"accuracy": 1.0},
                    "level_metrics": None,
                }
            )
            refresh_correction_run(db, case["correction"])
            correction_id = case["correction"].id
            base_workflow_id = case["base_workflow"].id
            candidate_workflow_id = candidate.id
            db.commit()

        def override_db():
            with SessionForTest() as db:
                yield db

        operator = SimpleNamespace(username="ops", role="admin", active=True)
        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: operator
        app.dependency_overrides[admin_user] = lambda: operator
        client = TestClient(app)

        response = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}/decision",
            json={"decision": "approved", "note": "候选回归通过，保留人工启用"},
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["candidate_workflow_version_id"] == (
            candidate_workflow_id
        )
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            binding = db.scalar(
                select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key
                    == "space_image"
                )
            )
            assert correction is not None and correction.status == "approved"
            assert binding is not None
            assert binding.workflow_version_id == base_workflow_id
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_candidate_regression_preserves_source_online_transport(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app import workflow_correction as wc
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            case = _seed_case(db)
            source = case["source_run"]
            frozen = json.loads(source.execution_snapshot_json)
            frozen["inference_mode"] = "online"
            source.execution_snapshot_json = canonical_json(frozen)
            analysis = wc.analyze_workflow_corrections(db, source.id, [case["source_item"].id])
            wc.compose_candidate_workflow(db, case["base_workflow"].id,
                wc.recommend_component_versions(db, analysis), case["correction"].id, "ops")
            received = {}
            def create(_db, **kwargs):
                received.update(kwargs)
                return SimpleNamespace(id=999999, execution_snapshot_json="{}")
            monkeypatch.setattr(wc, "create_native_baseline_run", create)
            wc.start_single_candidate_regression(db, case["correction"], "ops")
            assert received["inference_mode"] == "online"
            assert json.loads(source.execution_snapshot_json) == frozen
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_workflow_capacity_probe.py =====
"""Smoke gate for the zero-fee native workflow capacity artifact."""
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def test_probe_completes_native_runs_with_real_settlement_and_loopback_only():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    result = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, str(root / 'scripts/probe-native-workflow-capacity.py'),
         '--slots', '2', '--items', '4', '--wait-ms', '150', '--timeout', '60'],
        cwd=root, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=90,
    )
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stdout + result.stderr
    report = json.loads(result.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
    assert report['runs'] == {'succeeded': 4}
    assert report['invocations'] == {'succeeded': 4}
    assert report['attempts'] == {'succeeded': 16}
    assert report['provider_requests'] == 4
    assert report['provider_peak'] == 2
    assert report['duplicate_invocations'] == 0
    assert report['settled_media'] == 4
    assert report['unreleased_quota_leases'] == 0
    assert report['external_connect_attempts'] == 0
    assert report['database_kind'] == 'disposable_sqlite'
    assert report['transactions_surviving_entire_provider_wait'] == 0
    assert report['acceptance_passed'] is True

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_workflow_equivalence.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.category_evaluation_v3_revisions import revision_bundle
from app.database import Base
from app.dimension_deduction_bridge import empty_deduction_output
from app.models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.seed import seed_defaults


def _fixture(bundle: dict, *, primary_category: str, track_key: str) -> dict:
    precheck = {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "in_scope",
            "primary_category": primary_category,
            "primary_confidence": 0.96,
        },
        "production_fields": {
            "reason": [],
            "trait": "实景照片",
            "style": "现代",
        },
        "hard_defects": [],
    }
    dimension_output = empty_deduction_output(
        bundle["subcategory_dimensions"][track_key]
    )
    dimension_output.update(
        {
            "aesthetic_score": 86,
            "aesthetic_evidence": ["主体清晰，构图与材质关系稳定"],
            "aesthetic_confidence": 0.91,
        }
    )
    dimension_output.pop("warning", None)
    corrected = deepcopy(dimension_output)
    corrected["aesthetic_score"] = 76
    corrected["aesthetic_evidence"] = ["人工确认局部质感与完成度应下调"]
    return {
        "v3_context": bundle,
        "precheck": precheck,
        "dimension_output": dimension_output,
        "corrected_dimension_output": corrected,
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("category_key", "primary_category", "track_key"),
    [
        ("inspiration_image", "建筑设计", "class_one"),
        ("model_3d_su", "家装", "space_building"),
    ],
)
def test_native_workflow_matches_legacy_authoritative_fixture_and_correction(
    tmp_path,
    category_key: str,
    primary_category: str,
    track_key: str,
) -> None:
    from app.native_workflow_migration import (
        compare_legacy_and_native_fixture,
        ensure_native_category_workflows,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'{category_key}.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    display_name="灵感图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                    preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="system:test",
                )
            )
            seed_defaults(db)
            ensure_native_category_workflows(
                db,
                category_key,
                actor="system:native-workflow-migration",
            )
            active_revision = db.scalar(
                select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).where(
                    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key,
                    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.status == "active",
                )
            )
            assert active_revision is not None
            mapping = db.scalar(
                select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
                    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id
                    == active_revision.id
                )
            )
            assert mapping is not None
            workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, mapping.workflow_version_id)
            assert workflow is not None

            report = compare_legacy_and_native_fixture(
                category_key,
                _fixture(
                    revision_bundle(active_revision),
                    primary_category=primary_category,
                    track_key=track_key,
                ),
                workflow,
            )

            assert report.ok is True
            assert report.mismatches == ()
            assert report.legacy_hash == report.native_hash
            assert {
                "precheck",
                "redline",
                "track",
                "media_adjustment",
                "quality_cap_exception",
                "dimension_scores",
                "anchor",
                "score",
                "level",
                "confidence",
                "needs_review",
                "key_fields",
                "correction_result",
            } <= set(report.compared_fields)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_historical_rerun_uses_only_the_frozen_legacy_mapping(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_workflow_migration import (
        NativeWorkflowMigrationError,
        ensure_native_category_workflows,
        resolve_legacy_native_workflow,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'historical-rerun.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    display_name="灵感图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                    preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="system:test",
                )
            )
            seed_defaults(db)
            ensure_native_category_workflows(
                db,
                "inspiration_image",
                actor="system:native-workflow-migration",
            )
            revision = db.scalar(
                select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).where(
                    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key
                    == "inspiration_image",
                    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.status == "active",
                )
            )
            assert revision is not None

            resolved = resolve_legacy_native_workflow(
                db,
                legacy_revision_id=revision.id,
                category_key="inspiration_image",
            )
            assert resolved.version == f"native-r{revision.revision}"
            assert resolved.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"

            mapping = db.scalar(
                select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
                    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id
                    == revision.id
                )
            )
            assert mapping is not None
            db.delete(mapping)
            db.flush()
            with pytest.raises(NativeWorkflowMigrationError) as missing:
                resolve_legacy_native_workflow(
                    db,
                    legacy_revision_id=revision.id,
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                )
            assert missing.value.code == "native_historical_rerun_read_only"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_historical_revision_api_rerun_automatically_uses_native_mapping() -> None:
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user
    from app.models import Asset, BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, User
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        user = User(
            username="native-history-ops",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="运营",
            is_admin=True,
        )
        db.add_all(
            [
                user,
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    display_name="灵感图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                    preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="system:test",
                ),
            ]
        )
        seed_defaults(db)
        ensure_native_category_workflows(
            db,
            "inspiration_image",
            actor="system:native-workflow-migration",
        )
        revision = db.scalar(
            select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).where(
                CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == "inspiration_image",
                CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.status == "active",
            )
        )
        assert revision is not None
        mapping = db.scalar(
            select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
                LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id == revision.id
            )
        )
        assert mapping is not None
        asset = Asset(
            original_name="historical.jpg",
            stored_name="historical.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=100,
            sha256="e" * 64,
            category_key="inspiration_image",
        )
        db.add(asset)
        baseline = BaselineSet(
            category_key="inspiration_image",
            name="历史版本原生重跑",
            description="",
            default_expected_level="L2",
            fingerprint="f" * 64,
            created_by=user.username,
        )
        db.add(baseline)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level="L2",
                expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
                truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
                ),
            )
        )
        db.commit()

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
        response = TestClient(app).post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{baseline.id}/runs",
            json={"candidate_revision_id": revision.id},
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, response.json()["id"])
        assert run is not None
        assert run.execution_engine == "native_workflow"
        assert run.workflow_version_id == mapping.workflow_version_id
        assert response.json()["job_ids"] == []
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("category_key", "primary_category", "track_key"),
    [
        ("inspiration_image", "建筑设计", "class_one"),
        ("model_3d_su", "家装", "space_building"),
    ],
)
def test_existing_category_runs_native_regression_correction_candidate_loop(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    category_key: str,
    primary_category: str,
    track_key: str,
) -> None:
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.baseline_correction_orchestration import refresh_correction_run
    from app.evaluation_components import save_component_working_copy
    from app.models import (
        Asset,
        BaselineCorrectionRun,
        BaselineRegressionRun,
        BaselineSet,
        BaselineSetItem,
        CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
        EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
        EvaluationResult,
        ProductionRun,
        WorkflowNodeAsset,
    )
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows
    from app.worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring
    from app.workflow_correction import (
        analyze_workflow_corrections,
        append_node_correction_and_replay,
        compose_candidate_workflow,
        recommend_component_versions,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once
    from app.evaluation_v3_pipeline import recompute_qualified_v3

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'{category_key}-loop.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    display_name="灵感图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                    preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="system:test",
                )
            )
            seed_defaults(db)
            ensure_native_category_workflows(
                db,
                category_key,
                actor="system:native-workflow-migration",
            )
            active_revision = db.scalar(
                select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).where(
                    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key,
                    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.status == "active",
                )
            )
            assert active_revision is not None
            mapping = db.scalar(
                select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
                    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id
                    == active_revision.id
                )
            )
            assert mapping is not None
            workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, mapping.workflow_version_id)
            assert workflow is not None
            fixture = _fixture(
                revision_bundle(active_revision),
                primary_category=primary_category,
                track_key=track_key,
            )
            original = recompute_qualified_v3(
                v3_context=fixture["v3_context"],
                precheck=fixture["precheck"],
                dimension_output=fixture["dimension_output"],
            )
            expected = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(
                original, precheck=fixture["precheck"]
            )
            asset = Asset(
                original_name=f"{category_key}.jpg",
                stored_name=f"{category_key}.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=100,
                sha256=("a" if category_key == "inspiration_image" else "b") * 64,
                category_key=category_key,
            )
            db.add(asset)
            baseline_set = BaselineSet(
                category_key=category_key,
                name=f"{category_key} 原生闭环",
                description="",
                default_expected_level=expected["level"],
                fingerprint=("c" if category_key == "inspiration_image" else "d")
                * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(baseline_set)
            db.flush()
            baseline_item = BaselineSetItem(
                baseline_set_id=baseline_set.id,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_level=expected["level"],
                expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": expected["level"]}),
                truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {
                        "asset_id": asset.id,
                        "sha256": asset.sha256,
                        "workflow_fixture": {
                            "schema_version": "native-prompt-fixtures-v1",
                            "prompt_outputs": {
                                "prompt_a": {"precheck": fixture["precheck"]},
                                "prompt_b": {
                                    "dimension_output": fixture["dimension_output"]
                                },
                            },
                        },
                    }
                ),
            )
            db.add(baseline_item)
            db.flush()
            source = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline_set,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key=f"{category_key}-native-loop-source",
            )
            source_id = source.id
            source_item_id = source.items[0].id
            source_production_id = source.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as fixture_executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture_executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        for _ in range(30):
            if not process_runtime_step_once(f"{category_key}-worker"):
                break
        else:
            raise AssertionError("原生工作流未在 30 个步骤内结束")

        with sessions() as db:
            source = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, source_id)
            assert source is not None and source.status == "completed"
            source_item = source.items[0]
            assert source_item.status == "completed"
            evaluation = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationResult).where(
                    EvaluationResult.production_run_id == source_production_id
                )
            )
            assert evaluation is not None
            assert json.loads(evaluation.normalized_result_json)["level"] == expected[
                "level"
            ]
            source_snapshot = json.loads(source_item.result_snapshot_json)
            assert source_snapshot["predicted_level"] == expected["level"]
            assert source_snapshot["authoritative_score"] == expected["score"]
            assert source_snapshot["interpretation"]["status"] == "scored"
            assert source_snapshot["level_explanation"]["status"] == "available"
            assert source_snapshot["versions"] == {
                "model": evaluation.model_id,
                "prompt_a": evaluation.prompt_a_version,
                "prompt_b": evaluation.prompt_b_version,
                "rubric": evaluation.rubric_version,
                "engine": evaluation.engine_version,
            }
            correction_replay = append_node_correction_and_replay(
                db,
                run_id=source_production_id,
                node_instance_id="prompt_b",
                field_path="dimension_output.aesthetic_score",
                value=55,
                reason="人工确认美感基础分偏高",
                actor="运营甲",
                correction_key=f"{category_key}-prompt-b-score",
                evidence=[{"text": "完成度和材质表现不足"}],
            )
            assert "score_level_matcher_7" in correction_replay.affected_nodes
            corrected_snapshot = json.loads(source_item.result_snapshot_json)
            assert corrected_snapshot["predicted_level"] == (
                correction_replay.normalized_result["level"]
            )
            assert corrected_snapshot["authoritative_score"] == (
                correction_replay.normalized_result["score"]
            )
            assert corrected_snapshot["level_explanation"]["predicted_level"] == (
                correction_replay.normalized_result["level"]
            )
            assert corrected_snapshot["level_explanation"][
                "authoritative_score"
            ] == correction_replay.normalized_result["score"]

            prompt_asset = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == "prompt_b",
                )
            )
            assert prompt_asset is not None
            working = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
                    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == prompt_asset.id
                )
            )
            assert working is not None
            content = json.loads(working.content_json)
            content["system_prompt"] += "\n请吸收本轮人工纠偏证据。"
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=prompt_asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=working.lock_version,
                content=content,
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            correction = BaselineCorrectionRun(
                idempotency_key=f"{category_key}-native-loop-correction",
                baseline_run_id=source.id,
                category_key=category_key,
                selected_item_ids_json=canonical_json([source_item_id]),
                input_snapshot_json="{}",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(correction)
            db.flush()
            analysis = analyze_workflow_corrections(
                db, source.id, [source_item_id]
            )
            recommendations = recommend_component_versions(db, analysis)
            candidate = compose_candidate_workflow(
                db,
                workflow.id,
                recommendations,
                correction.id,
                "ops",
            )
            candidate_run = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db, correction, "ops"
            )
            correction_id = correction.id
            candidate_run_id = candidate_run.id
            db.commit()

        for _ in range(30):
            if not process_runtime_step_once(f"{category_key}-candidate-worker"):
                break
        else:
            raise AssertionError("候选原生工作流未在 30 个步骤内结束")

        with sessions() as db:
            correction = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            candidate_run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, candidate_run_id)
            assert correction is not None and candidate_run is not None
            assert candidate_run.status == "completed"
            refresh_correction_run(db, correction)
            assert correction.status == "awaiting_decision"
            assert correction.stage == "decision"
            report = json.loads(correction.report_json)["candidate_regression"]
            assert report["approval_allowed"] is True
            assert db.scalar(
                select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding).where(
                    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding.category_key == category_key
                )
            ) is None
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, source_production_id)
            assert production is not None and production.status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
